Regenerate via `python3 scripts/gates_support/render_cli_reference.py --repo-root .` -->\n\n# CLI Reference\n\n> Status: generated\n> Source of truth: `charness` parser and command-doc contract\n> Last verified: 2026-09-02\n\nThis file is generated from `./charness --help` and subcommand help output in the current checkout.\nOperational command payloads, including structured command failures, are emitted as a single YAML document on stdout; progress and unstructured fatal errors use stderr. Default responses are compact; `--detail` returns the full per-item records as one YAML document ([control plane](./control-plane.md#agent-readable-state)).\nHuman-readable summaries print the affordance line with the `NEXT:` prefix.\n\n## `charness`\n\n```text\nusage: charness [-h]\n                {init,update,doctor,version,uninstall,reset,task,hooks,train,catalog,capability,goal,tool,worktree}\n                ...\n\nThin charness CLI for managed local install, capability resolution, and\nexternal tool install/update/doctor flows.\n\npositional arguments:\n  {init,update,doctor,version,uninstall,reset,task,hooks,train,catalog,capability,goal,tool,worktree}\n    init                Bootstrap or refresh the managed local install\n                        surface, cloning the managed checkout first when it is\n                        missing.\n    update              Refresh the installed surface, pulling the managed\n                        checkout first by default.\n    doctor              Inspect the managed install surface and host-facing\n                        wrappers.\n    version             Report the current charness version and recorded\n                        install provenance.\n    uninstall           Remove the managed local install surface.\n    reset               Remove host plugin state for Codex and Claude while\n                        preserving the managed checkout and CLI.\n    task                Run or inspect a bounded task lane.\n    hooks               Inspect host hook intents.\n    train               Stack, verify, and fast-forward an explicit queue of\n                        local lane branches.\n    catalog             Inspect capability inventory, packaged consumer-\n                        validator adoption, or stale skill paths.\n    capability          Resolve repo-local logical capabilities through\n                        `<repo-root>/.charness/local/capability.json` and\n                        inspect provider readiness.\n    goal                Inspect and resume issue-native Goal Runs without\n                        local goal-file state.\n    tool                Inspect, install, update, or sync external tool\n                        integrations that charness-managed skills depend on.\n    worktree            Create, inspect, prepare, and clean up git worktrees\n                        so mutate-phase work runs against installed\n                        dependencies and live hooks.\n\noptions:\n  -h, --help            show this help message and exit\n```\n\n## `charness init`\n\n```text\nusage: charness init [-h] [--home-root HOME_ROOT] [--repo-root REPO_ROOT]\n                     [--target-repo-root TARGET_REPO_ROOT]\n                     [--repo-url REPO_URL] [--plugin-root PLUGIN_ROOT]\n                     [--codex-marketplace-path CODEX_MARKETPLACE_PATH]\n                     [--claude-wrapper-path CLAUDE_WRAPPER_PATH]\n                     [--cli-path CLI_PATH] [--skip-cli-install]\n                     [--skip-claude-wrapper] [--detail]\n\noptions:\n  -h, --help            show this help message and exit\n  --home-root HOME_ROOT\n  --repo-root REPO_ROOT\n                        Use an explicit existing source checkout instead of\n                        the managed default checkout.\n  --target-repo-root TARGET_REPO_ROOT\n                        Optional repo to inspect for post-install charness\n                        onboarding. Defaults to the current working directory\n                        for init/doctor; update inspects only when this is\n                        explicit.\n  --repo-url REPO_URL\n  --plugin-root PLUGIN_ROOT\n  --codex-marketplace-path CODEX_MARKETPLACE_PATH\n  --claude-wrapper-path CLAUDE_WRAPPER_PATH\n  --cli-path CLI_PATH\n  --skip-cli-install\n  --skip-claude-wrapper\n  --detail              Emit the full diagnostic YAML payload instead of the\n                        default compact operational summary.\n```\n\n## `charness update`\n\n```text\nusage: charness update [-h] [--home-root HOME_ROOT] [--repo-root REPO_ROOT]\n                       [--target-repo-root TARGET_REPO_ROOT]\n                       [--repo-url REPO_URL] [--plugin-root PLUGIN_ROOT]\n                       [--codex-marketplace-path CODEX_MARKETPLACE_PATH]\n                       [--claude-wrapper-path CLAUDE_WRAPPER_PATH]\n                       [--cli-path CLI_PATH] [--skip-cli-install]\n                       [--skip-claude-wrapper] [--detail] [--no-pull]\n                       [--skip-codex-cache-refresh]\n                       [{all}]\n\npositional arguments:\n  {all}                 Also run `charness tool update` for all tracked\n                        external integrations after refreshing the charness\n                        install surface itself.\n\noptions:\n  -h, --help            show this help message and exit\n  --home-root HOME_ROOT\n  --repo-root REPO_ROOT\n                        Use an explicit existing source checkout instead of\n                        the managed default checkout.\n  --target-repo-root TARGET_REPO_ROOT\n                        Optional repo to inspect for post-install charness\n                        onboarding. Defaults to the current working directory\n                        for init/doctor; update inspects only when this is\n                        explicit.\n  --repo-url REPO_URL\n  --plugin-root PLUGIN_ROOT\n  --codex-marketplace-path CODEX_MARKETPLACE_PATH\n  --claude-wrapper-path CLAUDE_WRAPPER_PATH\n  --cli-path CLI_PATH\n  --skip-cli-install\n  --skip-claude-wrapper\n  --detail              Emit the full diagnostic YAML payload instead of the\n                        default compact operational summary.\n  --no-pull             Skip the default `git pull --ff-only` when the managed\n                        checkout already contains the exact source you want.\n  --skip-codex-cache-refresh\n                        Do not call Codex app-server `plugin/install` to\n                        refresh the enabled local plugin cache after updating\n                        the source plugin root.\n```\n\n## `charness doctor`\n\n```text\nusage: charness doctor [-h] [--home-root HOME_ROOT] [--repo-root REPO_ROOT]\n                       [--target-repo-root TARGET_REPO_ROOT]\n                       [--plugin-root PLUGIN_ROOT]\n                       [--codex-marketplace-path CODEX_MARKETPLACE_PATH]\n                       [--claude-wrapper-path CLAUDE_WRAPPER_PATH]\n                       [--cli-path CLI_PATH] [--next-action] [--write-state]\n                       [--detail]\n\noptions:\n  -h, --help            show this help message and exit\n  --home-root HOME_ROOT\n  --repo-root REPO_ROOT\n                        Inspect an explicit source checkout instead of the\n                        managed default checkout.\n  --target-repo-root TARGET_REPO_ROOT\n                        Optional repo to inspect for charness onboarding.\n                        Defaults to the current working directory.\n  --plugin-root PLUGIN_ROOT\n  --codex-marketplace-path CODEX_MARKETPLACE_PATH\n  --claude-wrapper-path CLAUDE_WRAPPER_PATH\n  --cli-path CLI_PATH\n  --next-action         Print only the current primary next action message.\n  --write-state         Persist the current doctor snapshot to the machine-\n                        local charness state directory for later proof\n                        comparison.\n  --detail              Emit the full diagnostic YAML payload instead of the\n                        default compact operational summary.\n```\n\n## `charness version`\n\n```text\nusage: charness version [-h] [--home-root HOME_ROOT] [--repo-root REPO_ROOT]\n                        [--cli-path CLI_PATH] [--verbose] [--check]\n\noptions:\n  -h, --help            show this help message and exit\n  --home-root HOME_ROOT\n  --repo-root REPO_ROOT\n                        Inspect an explicit source checkout instead of the\n                        managed default checkout.\n  --cli-path CLI_PATH\n  --verbose\n  --check               Refresh the cached latest-release check now instead of\n                        only showing recorded state.\n```\n\n## `charness uninstall`\n\n```text\nusage: charness uninstall [-h] [--home-root HOME_ROOT] [--repo-root REPO_ROOT]\n                          [--plugin-root PLUGIN_ROOT]\n                          [--codex-marketplace-path CODEX_MARKETPLACE_PATH]\n                          [--claude-wrapper-path CLAUDE_WRAPPER_PATH]\n                          [--cli-path CLI_PATH] [--delete-checkout]\n                          [--delete-cli]\n\noptions:\n  -h, --help            show this help message and exit\n  --home-root HOME_ROOT\n  --repo-root REPO_ROOT\n                        Use an explicit checkout path when `--delete-checkout`\n                        is set.\n  --plugin-root PLUGIN_ROOT\n  --codex-marketplace-path CODEX_MARKETPLACE_PATH\n  --claude-wrapper-path CLAUDE_WRAPPER_PATH\n  --cli-path CLI_PATH\n  --delete-checkout\n  --delete-cli\n```\n\n## `charness reset`\n\n```text\nusage: charness reset [-h] [--home-root HOME_ROOT] [--repo-root REPO_ROOT]\n                      [--plugin-root PLUGIN_ROOT]\n                      [--codex-marketplace-path CODEX_MARKETPLACE_PATH]\n                      [--claude-wrapper-path CLAUDE_WRAPPER_PATH]\n                      [--cli-path CLI_PATH]\n\noptions:\n  -h, --help            show this help message and exit\n  --home-root HOME_ROOT\n  --repo-root REPO_ROOT\n                        Use an explicit checkout path when removing host\n                        plugin state.\n  --plugin-root PLUGIN_ROOT\n  --codex-marketplace-path CODEX_MARKETPLACE_PATH\n  --claude-wrapper-path CLAUDE_WRAPPER_PATH\n  --cli-path CLI_PATH\n```\n\n## `charness task`\n\n```text\nusage: charness task [-h] {status,report,executors,steer,run,wait} ...\n\npositional arguments:\n  {status,report,executors,steer,run,wait}\n    status              Show one external task-run result, or list all task-\n                        run results.\n    report              Render the fixed-shape periodic status report from\n                        lane metrics, the friction log, and the decision\n                        ledger.\n    executors           Check executor executable availability without\n                        starting a lane.\n    steer               Queue a message for a running lane or amend its\n                        retained candidate scope.\n    run                 Run one independently delegable lane in a clean named\n                        worktree and emit a compact receipt.\n    wait                Block until named lanes reach a terminal status.\n\noptions:\n  -h, --help            show this help message and exit\n```\n\n## `charness task status`\n\n```text\nusage: charness task status [-h] [--repo-root REPO_ROOT] [--all-repos]\n                            [--commands]\n                            [task_id]\n\npositional arguments:\n  task_id\n\noptions:\n  -h, --help            show this help message and exit\n  --repo-root REPO_ROOT\n                        Parent repo whose external task-run runtime is read.\n  --all-repos           Report the compact running-task inventory across this\n                        repo's managed runtime tree.\n  --commands            With a task id, report that lane's longest commands,\n                        starts per command family, and phase times from its\n                        receive-time-stamped events log.\n```\n\n## `charness task report`\n\n```text\nusage: charness task report [-h] [--repo-root REPO_ROOT] [--window WINDOW]\n\noptions:\n  -h, --help            show this help message and exit\n  --repo-root REPO_ROOT\n                        Parent repo whose external task-run runtime is read.\n  --window WINDOW       Trailing window for the period (30m, 3h, 1d, or bare\n                        hours).\n```\n\n## `charness task executors`\n\n```text\nusage: charness task executors [-h] [--repo-root REPO_ROOT]\n\nResolve the configured executor executables without launching them. Provider\nquota remains unknown until an executor reports a usage limit.\n\noptions:\n  -h, --help            show this help message and exit\n  --repo-root REPO_ROOT\n                        Repo whose task-run environment is selected. Defaults\n                        to the current directory.\n```\n\n## `charness task steer`\n\n```text\nusage: charness task steer [-h] [--repo-root REPO_ROOT]\n                           (--message MESSAGE | --amend-scope AMEND_SCOPE)\n                           [--reason REASON] [--actor ACTOR]\n                           task_id\n\npositional arguments:\n  task_id\n\noptions:\n  -h, --help            show this help message and exit\n  --repo-root REPO_ROOT\n                        Parent repo whose external task-run runtime is read.\n  --message MESSAGE     Message to deliver at the next executor turn boundary.\n  --amend-scope AMEND_SCOPE\n                        Approve an additional scope and revalidate the same\n                        retained candidate; repeatable.\n  --reason REASON       Why this steer/amendment is issued; recorded as the\n                        envelope `reason` audit field.\n  --actor ACTOR         Who issues this steer/amendment; recorded as the\n                        envelope `actor` audit field.\n```\n\n## `charness task run`\n\n```text\nusage: charness task run [-h] [--repo-root REPO_ROOT] [--lane LANE]\n                         [--path PATH] [--branch BRANCH] [--base BASE] --scope\n                         SCOPE (--prompt PROMPT | --prompt-file PROMPT_FILE)\n                         [--executor EXECUTOR] --effort EFFORT\n                         [--task-id TASK_ID] [--prepare] [--require-change]\n                         [--skip-prepare] [--allow-no-change] [--report-only]\n                         [--self-review] [--critical-lane]\n                         [--acceptance-skeleton ACCEPTANCE_SKELETON]\n                         [--premise-check ID PREMISE DECISION]\n                         [--timeout-seconds TIMEOUT_SECONDS]\n                         [--no-progress-seconds NO_PROGRESS_SECONDS]\n                         [--dry-run] [--rules-file RULES_FILE]\n                         [--grant-writable GRANT_WRITABLE] [--detach]\n\nRun one independently delegable lane: shorthand derives a named branch, external worktree, task id, and HEAD base; the explicit form remains available for diagnostics. The parent worktree must be clean; the parent orchestrator owns parallel fan-out and integration.\n\nExit codes:\n  0 success\n  1 failed\n  2 premise-blocked\n  3 validated-partial\n  4 executor-unavailable\n  5 completed-needs-review\n\noptions:\n  -h, --help            show this help message and exit\n  --repo-root REPO_ROOT\n                        Clean parent repo from which the linked worktree is\n                        created. Defaults to the current working directory.\n  --lane LANE           Safe lane id; derives the task id, task/<id> branch,\n                        and external worktree (HEAD base unless --base is\n                        given).\n  --path PATH           New linked worktree path outside the parent repo\n                        (explicit form).\n  --branch BRANCH       Named local branch for the new worktree (explicit\n                        form).\n  --base BASE           Commit/ref the new worktree starts from (required with\n                        --path/--branch; overrides the lane HEAD base with\n                        --lane).\n  --scope SCOPE         Repository-relative candidate path or quoted glob;\n                        repeatable, and a `{a,b}` group expands to one scope\n                        per alternative. Existing directories include\n                        descendants; a path absent from the base is an exact\n                        file, so enumerate planned files in a new directory. A\n                        glob that matches nothing warns and admits lane-\n                        created matching paths on refresh.\n  --prompt PROMPT       Implementation instructions passed to the lane\n                        executor.\n  --prompt-file PROMPT_FILE\n                        Read implementation instructions from this file.\n  --executor EXECUTOR   Ordered lane executors, comma-separated (for example\n                        codex,muse); usage-limit failures fall through in\n                        order. Default: codex. Effort must fit every listed\n                        executor.\n  --effort EFFORT       Orchestrator-selected reasoning effort: medium, xhigh,\n                        or max for codex; medium, high, xhigh, or max for\n                        muse.\n  --task-id TASK_ID     Optional receipt/log identifier for explicit runs;\n                        shorthand derives it from --lane.\n  --prepare             Run the worktree adapter prepare step before the lane\n                        executor.\n  --require-change      Fail unless the candidate changes at least one path.\n  --skip-prepare        Shorthand diagnostic opt-out: skip the default\n                        preparation step.\n  --allow-no-change     Shorthand diagnostic opt-out: allow an unchanged\n                        candidate.\n  --report-only         Report-only lane: force require-change off, treat the\n                        delivered stdout report as the terminal artifact, and\n                        never classify parent progress as a writer conflict.\n                        Cannot be combined with --require-change.\n  --self-review         Run an explicit read-only self-review pass; findings\n                        do not provide approval.\n  --critical-lane       Require a committed failing acceptance skeleton before\n                        launch and green at completion.\n  --acceptance-skeleton ACCEPTANCE_SKELETON\n                        Repository-relative pytest file that must fail before\n                        launch and pass before completion.\n  --premise-check ID PREMISE DECISION\n                        Premise to check before launch and the decision a\n                        BLOCKED result needs; repeatable.\n  --timeout-seconds TIMEOUT_SECONDS\n                        Optional hard wall-clock cap per executor invocation\n                        (each preemption or resume starts a new one); none by\n                        default. A lane is bounded by progress instead:\n                        CHARNESS_TASK_RUN_PROGRESS_WINDOW_SECONDS (default\n                        1200) asks for a checkpoint after a window with no\n                        commit, file change, or new command result and stops\n                        the lane at a later empty check.\n  --no-progress-seconds NO_PROGRESS_SECONDS\n                        First-edit budget for require-change lanes: seconds\n                        from CONTRACT-READ to the first EDITING or scoped\n                        diff; 0 turns this stop off. Overrides\n                        CHARNESS_TASK_RUN_NO_PROGRESS_SECONDS and is recorded\n                        in progress_guard. Separate from the progress window.\n  --dry-run             Validate inputs and show the planned lane without\n                        creating or running it.\n  --rules-file RULES_FILE\n                        Standing-rule file the lane executor must read by\n                        reference; repeatable. Listed by absolute path in the\n                        lane prompt, never inlined, and never read by scope\n                        evidence.\n  --grant-writable GRANT_WRITABLE\n                        Absolute host-state directory a codex lane may write\n                        (mapped to codex --add-dir); repeatable. Refused for\n                        muse lanes and for grants covering the repo root,\n                        $HOME, or /.\n  --detach              Launch the lane detached and return once its carrier\n                        has started (exit 0, printing the task id and result\n                        path), or with the task-run exit code of a preflight\n                        or launch failure. Never returns 0 for a lane that did\n                        not start. Cannot be combined with --dry-run.\n```\n\n## `charness task wait`\n\n```text\nusage: charness task wait [-h] [--repo-root REPO_ROOT] [--any]\n                          [--timeout-seconds TIMEOUT_SECONDS]\n                          task_ids [task_ids ...]\n\nBlock until all (or with --any, the first) of the named lanes reach a terminal status; print task_id and status for each finished lane. Exit with the finished lane's task-run exit code (0 success, 1 failed, 2 premise-blocked, 3 validated-partial, 4 executor-unavailable, 5 completed-needs-review); with several lanes, the first non-success in CLI order decides. An already-terminal lane returns immediately.\n\npositional arguments:\n  task_ids              Lane task ids to wait for.\n\noptions:\n  -h, --help            show this help message and exit\n  --repo-root REPO_ROOT\n                        Parent repo whose external task-run runtime is read.\n  --any                 Return when the first named lane ends; the others keep\n                        running.\n  --timeout-seconds TIMEOUT_SECONDS\n                        Bound the wait in seconds; 0 waits indefinitely.\n```\n\n## `charness hooks`\n\n```text\nusage: charness hooks [-h] {status} ...\n\npositional arguments:\n  {status}\n    status    Report every host-hook intent per host.\n\noptions:\n  -h, --help  show this help message and exit\n```\n\n## `charness hooks status`\n\n```text\nusage: charness hooks status [-h] [--repo-root REPO_ROOT]\n                             [--adapter-file ADAPTER_FILE]\n\nReport every host-hook intent (the skill-anchor edit guard and the three command-time orchestration guards) per host: declared intent, installed actual, and whether they agree. Exit 0 when every intent is in sync, 1 otherwise.\n\noptions:\n  -h, --help            show this help message and exit\n  --repo-root REPO_ROOT\n                        Repo whose hook state is read. Defaults to the current\n                        directory.\n  --adapter-file ADAPTER_FILE\n                        Adapter YAML declaring host-hook intents; absent means\n                        every intent disabled.\n```\n\n## `charness train`\n\n```text\nusage: charness train [-h] [--repo-root REPO_ROOT] [--main MAIN]\n                      [--profile PROFILE] [--stats] [--window WINDOW]\n                      [branches ...]\n\npositional arguments:\n  branches              Local lane branches in queue order (empty with\n                        --stats).\n\noptions:\n  -h, --help            show this help message and exit\n  --repo-root REPO_ROOT\n                        Repository whose local main branch and lane branches\n                        are integrated.\n  --main MAIN           Local branch to guard and fast-forward (default:\n                        main).\n  --profile PROFILE     Optional verify profile; defaults to .agents/train-\n                        verify.yaml or built-in standing pytest.\n  --stats               Report train throughput (landings, queue wait, waste)\n                        instead of landing.\n  --window WINDOW       Trailing window for --stats (like 30m, 3h, 1d;\n                        default: 3h).\n```\n\n## `charness catalog`\n\n```text\nusage: charness catalog [-h] {list,refresh,resolve-skill-path} ...\n\npositional arguments:\n  {list,refresh,resolve-skill-path}\n    list                Read capability and packaged consumer-validator\n                        inventory without writing artifacts.\n    refresh             Write the canonical capability catalog current-pointer\n                        artifacts.\n    resolve-skill-path  Resolve a stale host-reported skill path after plugin\n                        cache rotation.\n\noptions:\n  -h, --help            show this help message and exit\n```\n\n## `charness catalog list`\n\n```text\nusage: charness catalog list [-h] --repo-root REPO_ROOT [--summary]\n\noptions:\n  -h, --help            show this help message and exit\n  --repo-root REPO_ROOT\n  --summary             Project support/integration inventory while retaining\n                        the validator catalog contract.\n```\n\n## `charness catalog refresh`\n\n```text\nusage: charness catalog refresh [-h] --repo-root REPO_ROOT\n\noptions:\n  -h, --help            show this help message and exit\n  --repo-root REPO_ROOT\n```\n\n## `charness catalog resolve-skill-path`\n\n```text\nusage: charness catalog resolve-skill-path [-h] --repo-root REPO_ROOT\n                                           --skill-id SKILL_ID --reported-path\n                                           REPORTED_PATH [--home HOME]\n                                           [--codex-home CODEX_HOME]\n                                           [--marketplace MARKETPLACE]\n                                           [--plugin PLUGIN]\n\noptions:\n  -h, --help            show this help message and exit\n  --repo-root REPO_ROOT\n  --skill-id SKILL_ID\n  --reported-path REPORTED_PATH\n  --home HOME\n  --codex-home CODEX_HOME\n  --marketplace MARKETPLACE\n  --plugin PLUGIN\n```\n\n## `charness capability`\n\n```text\nusage: charness capability [-h] {init,resolve,doctor,env,explain} ...\n\npositional arguments:\n  {init,resolve,doctor,env,explain}\n    init                Scaffold repo-local capability config\n                        (`.charness/local/capability.json` +\n                        `.charness/capability.example.json`) and update\n                        `.gitignore`.\n    resolve             Resolve one logical capability for the current repo\n                        into a profile and provider.\n    doctor              Resolve one logical capability and inspect the\n                        underlying provider state.\n    env                 Emit shell exports that alias runtime env names from\n                        non-secret source env names declared in the repo-local\n                        capability config.\n    explain             Explain which logical capabilities a public skill may\n                        need and what the current repo adapter adds.\n\noptions:\n  -h, --help            show this help message and exit\n```\n\n## `charness capability init`\n\n```text\nusage: charness capability init [-h] [--target-repo-root TARGET_REPO_ROOT]\n                                [--force]\n\noptions:\n  -h, --help            show this help message and exit\n  --target-repo-root TARGET_REPO_ROOT\n                        Scaffold capability config under this target repo.\n                        Defaults to the current working directory.\n  --force\n```\n\n## `charness capability resolve`\n\n```text\nusage: charness capability resolve [-h] [--repo-root REPO_ROOT]\n                                   [--repo-url REPO_URL]\n                                   [--target-repo-root TARGET_REPO_ROOT]\n                                   logical_id\n\npositional arguments:\n  logical_id\n\noptions:\n  -h, --help            show this help message and exit\n  --repo-root REPO_ROOT\n                        Use an explicit charness source checkout instead of\n                        the managed default checkout.\n  --repo-url REPO_URL\n  --target-repo-root TARGET_REPO_ROOT\n                        Resolve repo-local capability config for this target\n                        repo. Defaults to the current working directory.\n```\n\n## `charness capability doctor`\n\n```text\nusage: charness capability doctor [-h] [--repo-root REPO_ROOT]\n                                  [--repo-url REPO_URL]\n                                  [--target-repo-root TARGET_REPO_ROOT]\n                                  logical_id\n\npositional arguments:\n  logical_id\n\noptions:\n  -h, --help            show this help message and exit\n  --repo-root REPO_ROOT\n                        Use an explicit charness source checkout instead of\n                        the managed default checkout.\n  --repo-url REPO_URL\n  --target-repo-root TARGET_REPO_ROOT\n                        Resolve repo-local capability config for this target\n                        repo. Defaults to the current working directory.\n```\n\n## `charness capability env`\n\n```text\nusage: charness capability env [-h] [--repo-root REPO_ROOT]\n                               [--repo-url REPO_URL]\n                               [--target-repo-root TARGET_REPO_ROOT]\n                               logical_id\n\npositional arguments:\n  logical_id\n\noptions:\n  -h, --help            show this help message and exit\n  --repo-root REPO_ROOT\n                        Use an explicit charness source checkout instead of\n                        the managed default checkout.\n  --repo-url REPO_URL\n  --target-repo-root TARGET_REPO_ROOT\n                        Resolve repo-local capability config for this target\n                        repo. Defaults to the current working directory.\n```\n\n## `charness capability explain`\n\n```text\nusage: charness capability explain [-h] [--repo-root REPO_ROOT]\n                                   [--repo-url REPO_URL]\n                                   [--target-repo-root TARGET_REPO_ROOT]\n                                   skill_id\n\npositional arguments:\n  skill_id\n\noptions:\n  -h, --help            show this help message and exit\n  --repo-root REPO_ROOT\n                        Use an explicit charness source checkout instead of\n                        the managed default checkout.\n  --repo-url REPO_URL\n  --target-repo-root TARGET_REPO_ROOT\n                        Inspect repo-local adapter context for this target\n                        repo. Defaults to the current working directory.\n```\n\n## `charness goal`\n\n```text\nusage: charness goal [-h] {run} ...\n\npositional arguments:\n  {run}\n    run       Read a provider-backed Goal Run and select its next executable\n              child; no local artifact path is accepted.\n\noptions:\n  -h, --help  show this help message and exit\n```\n\n## `charness goal run`\n\n```text\nusage: charness goal run [-h] [--repo-root REPO_ROOT] --objective OBJECTIVE\n                         [--home-root HOME_ROOT] [--repo-url REPO_URL]\n                         [--charness-checkout CHARNESS_CHECKOUT]\n\nResume a provider-backed Goal Run; no local artifact path is accepted.\n\noptions:\n  -h, --help            show this help message and exit\n  --repo-root REPO_ROOT\n                        Target repository containing the Goal Run. Defaults to\n                        the current working directory.\n  --objective OBJECTIVE\n                        Exact issue-native objective, for example `/goal #N`.\n  --home-root HOME_ROOT\n  --repo-url REPO_URL\n  --charness-checkout CHARNESS_CHECKOUT\n                        Explicit Charness source checkout containing the\n                        current Goal Run pickup helper. Defaults to this CLI\n                        checkout or the managed checkout.\n```\n\n## `charness tool`\n\n```text\nusage: charness tool [-h] {doctor,repair,sync-support,install,update} ...\n\npositional arguments:\n  {doctor,repair,sync-support,install,update}\n    doctor              Write machine-readable doctor state for one or more\n                        external tools.\n    repair              Run post-hoc repair actions for external tool runtime\n                        drift, then refresh doctor state.\n    sync-support        Refresh cache-backed support skill materialization for\n                        one or more external tools.\n    install             Attempt tool installation where the manifest allows\n                        it, otherwise persist install guidance and doctor\n                        state.\n    update              Attempt manifest-declared external tool updates, then\n                        refresh support skill materialization and doctor\n                        state.\n\noptions:\n  -h, --help            show this help message and exit\n```\n\n## `charness tool doctor`\n\n```text\nusage: charness tool doctor [-h] [--home-root HOME_ROOT]\n                            [--repo-root REPO_ROOT] [--repo-url REPO_URL]\n                            [--plugin-root PLUGIN_ROOT] [--detail]\n                            [--no-write-locks]\n                            [tool_ids ...]\n\npositional arguments:\n  tool_ids\n\noptions:\n  -h, --help            show this help message and exit\n  --home-root HOME_ROOT\n  --repo-root REPO_ROOT\n                        Use an explicit existing source checkout instead of\n                        the managed default checkout.\n  --repo-url REPO_URL\n  --plugin-root PLUGIN_ROOT\n                        Installed plugin root where upstream support skills\n                        are materialized.\n  --detail              Emit the full diagnostic YAML payload instead of the\n                        default compact operational summary.\n  --no-write-locks      Skip updating integrations/locks/*.json when you only\n                        want a read-only probe.\n```\n\n## `charness tool repair`\n\n```text\nusage: charness tool repair [-h] [--home-root HOME_ROOT]\n                            [--repo-root REPO_ROOT] [--repo-url REPO_URL]\n                            [--plugin-root PLUGIN_ROOT] [--detail] [--execute]\n                            [tool_ids ...]\n\nRun repo-owned post-hoc repair actions for external tool runtime drift, then\nrefresh doctor state. For agent-browser this is mitigation only; invocation-\nbound Chrome/profile teardown remains upstream/unproven.\n\npositional arguments:\n  tool_ids\n\noptions:\n  -h, --help            show this help message and exit\n  --home-root HOME_ROOT\n  --repo-root REPO_ROOT\n                        Use an explicit existing source checkout instead of\n                        the managed default checkout.\n  --repo-url REPO_URL\n  --plugin-root PLUGIN_ROOT\n                        Installed plugin root where upstream support skills\n                        are materialized.\n  --detail              Emit the full diagnostic YAML payload instead of the\n                        default compact operational summary.\n  --execute             Execute the repair. Defaults to a dry-run preview.\n```\n\n## `charness tool sync-support`\n\n```text\nusage: charness tool sync-support [-h] [--home-root HOME_ROOT]\n                                  [--repo-root REPO_ROOT]\n                                  [--repo-url REPO_URL]\n                                  [--plugin-root PLUGIN_ROOT] [--detail]\n                                  [--upstream-checkout UPSTREAM_CHECKOUT]\n                                  [--dry-run]\n                                  [tool_ids ...]\n\npositional arguments:\n  tool_ids\n\noptions:\n  -h, --help            show this help message and exit\n  --home-root HOME_ROOT\n  --repo-root REPO_ROOT\n                        Use an explicit existing source checkout instead of\n                        the managed default checkout.\n  --repo-url REPO_URL\n  --plugin-root PLUGIN_ROOT\n                        Installed plugin root where upstream support skills\n                        are materialized.\n  --detail              Emit the full diagnostic YAML payload instead of the\n                        default compact operational summary.\n  --upstream-checkout UPSTREAM_CHECKOUT\n  --dry-run\n```\n\n## `charness tool install`\n\n```text\nusage: charness tool install [-h] [--home-root HOME_ROOT]\n                             [--repo-root REPO_ROOT] [--repo-url REPO_URL]\n                             [--plugin-root PLUGIN_ROOT] [--detail]\n                             [--upstream-checkout UPSTREAM_CHECKOUT]\n                             [--dry-run] [--skip-sync-support]\n                             [--recommend-for-skill RECOMMEND_FOR_SKILL]\n                             [--recommendation-role {runtime,validation}]\n                             [--next-skill-id NEXT_SKILL_ID]\n                             [tool_ids ...]\n\npositional arguments:\n  tool_ids\n\noptions:\n  -h, --help            show this help message and exit\n  --home-root HOME_ROOT\n  --repo-root REPO_ROOT\n                        Use an explicit existing source checkout instead of\n                        the managed default checkout.\n  --repo-url REPO_URL\n  --plugin-root PLUGIN_ROOT\n                        Installed plugin root where upstream support skills\n                        are materialized.\n  --detail              Emit the full diagnostic YAML payload instead of the\n                        default compact operational summary.\n  --upstream-checkout UPSTREAM_CHECKOUT\n  --dry-run\n  --skip-sync-support   Skip support skill rematerialization after install\n                        guidance or execution.\n  --recommend-for-skill RECOMMEND_FOR_SKILL\n                        Install tools declared as supporting a public skill\n                        instead of passing explicit tool ids.\n  --recommendation-role {runtime,validation}\n                        Install tools with a recommendation role, optionally\n                        scoped by --next-skill-id.\n  --next-skill-id NEXT_SKILL_ID\n                        Public skill id used with --recommendation-role;\n                        defaults to quality.\n```\n\nExamples\n\n```bash\ncharness tool install --recommendation-role validation --next-skill-id quality\n```\n\n## `charness tool update`\n\n```text\nusage: charness tool update [-h] [--home-root HOME_ROOT]\n                            [--repo-root REPO_ROOT] [--repo-url REPO_URL]\n                            [--plugin-root PLUGIN_ROOT] [--detail]\n                            [--upstream-checkout UPSTREAM_CHECKOUT]\n                            [--dry-run] [--skip-sync-support]\n                            [tool_ids ...]\n\npositional arguments:\n  tool_ids\n\noptions:\n  -h, --help            show this help message and exit\n  --home-root HOME_ROOT\n  --repo-root REPO_ROOT\n                        Use an explicit existing source checkout instead of\n                        the managed default checkout.\n  --repo-url REPO_URL\n  --plugin-root PLUGIN_ROOT\n                        Installed plugin root where upstream support skills\n                        are materialized.\n  --detail              Emit the full diagnostic YAML payload instead of the\n                        default compact operational summary.\n  --upstream-checkout UPSTREAM_CHECKOUT\n  --dry-run\n  --skip-sync-support   Skip support skill rematerialization after update.\n```\n\n## `charness worktree`\n\n```text\nusage: charness worktree [-h]\n                         {create,add,exec,doctor,prepare,audit,cleanup} ...\n\npositional arguments:\n  {create,add,exec,doctor,prepare,audit,cleanup}\n    create              Create a git worktree, then run readiness doctor and\n                        optional prepare.\n    add                 Alias for `create`: wrap `git worktree add` with\n                        readiness doctor and optional prepare.\n    exec                Run one command in an isolated worktree with external\n                        runtime caches.\n    doctor              Probe worktree readiness (isolation, hooksPath,\n                        lefthook shim resolution, husky directory, manifest\n                        checks).\n    prepare             Run the worktree adapter's prepare commands and re-\n                        validate readiness.\n    audit               Survey all worktrees registered to the repository and\n                        classify primary/active/prunable/stale.\n    cleanup             Safely remove a registered git worktree and optionally\n                        delete its merged local branch.\n\noptions:\n  -h, --help            show this help message and exit\n```\n\n## `charness worktree create`\n\n```text\nusage: charness worktree create [-h] [--repo-root REPO_ROOT] --path PATH\n                                [--branch BRANCH] [--base BASE] [--detach]\n                                [--prepare] [--dry-run] [--force]\n                                [--ephemeral | --owned]\n                                [--home-root HOME_ROOT]\n                                [--charness-checkout CHARNESS_CHECKOUT]\n\noptions:\n  -h, --help            show this help message and exit\n  --repo-root REPO_ROOT\n                        Repo root that the worktree should be created under.\n                        Defaults to the current working directory.\n  --path PATH           Path for the new git worktree.\n  --branch BRANCH       Create a new local branch for the worktree.\n  --base BASE           Base ref passed to `git worktree add` after the path.\n  --detach              Create a detached-HEAD worktree.\n  --prepare             Run readiness prepare after creation.\n  --dry-run             Print the planned git command without creating the\n                        worktree.\n  --force               Pass --force to `git worktree add`.\n  --ephemeral           Mark the worktree disposable: create reclaims expired\n                        leftovers and caps residue.\n  --owned               Keep the worktree until `charness worktree cleanup`;\n                        never auto-removed.\n  --home-root HOME_ROOT\n                        Home root used to locate the managed charness checkout\n                        when the entrypoint is a PATH shim.\n  --charness-checkout CHARNESS_CHECKOUT\n                        Explicit charness source checkout to load worktree\n                        helpers from. Defaults to the embedded or managed\n                        checkout.\n```\n\n## `charness worktree add`\n\n```text\nusage: charness worktree add [-h] [--repo-root REPO_ROOT] --path PATH\n                             [--branch BRANCH] [--base BASE] [--detach]\n                             [--prepare] [--dry-run] [--force]\n                             [--ephemeral | --owned] [--home-root HOME_ROOT]\n                             [--charness-checkout CHARNESS_CHECKOUT]\n\noptions:\n  -h, --help            show this help message and exit\n  --repo-root REPO_ROOT\n                        Repo root that the worktree should be created under.\n                        Defaults to the current working directory.\n  --path PATH           Path for the new git worktree.\n  --branch BRANCH       Create a new local branch for the worktree.\n  --base BASE           Base ref passed to `git worktree add` after the path.\n  --detach              Create a detached-HEAD worktree.\n  --prepare             Run readiness prepare after creation.\n  --dry-run             Print the planned git command without creating the\n                        worktree.\n  --force               Pass --force to `git worktree add`.\n  --ephemeral           Mark the worktree disposable: create reclaims expired\n                        leftovers and caps residue.\n  --owned               Keep the worktree until `charness worktree cleanup`;\n                        never auto-removed.\n  --home-root HOME_ROOT\n                        Home root used to locate the managed charness checkout\n                        when the entrypoint is a PATH shim.\n  --charness-checkout CHARNESS_CHECKOUT\n                        Explicit charness source checkout to load worktree\n                        helpers from. Defaults to the embedded or managed\n                        checkout.\n```\n\n## `charness worktree exec`\n\n```text\nusage: charness worktree exec [-h] [--repo-root REPO_ROOT] [--allow-main]\n                              [--home-root HOME_ROOT]\n                              [--charness-checkout CHARNESS_CHECKOUT]\n                              ...\n\nRun one command in an isolated worktree with external runtime caches.\n\npositional arguments:\n  command               Command after `--`.\n\noptions:\n  -h, --help            show this help message and exit\n  --repo-root REPO_ROOT\n                        Worktree in which to run the command. Defaults to the\n                        current working directory.\n  --allow-main          Allow an intentional command in the primary worktree;\n                        parent writes are then possible.\n  --home-root HOME_ROOT\n                        Home root used to locate the managed charness checkout\n                        when the entrypoint is a PATH shim.\n  --charness-checkout CHARNESS_CHECKOUT\n                        Explicit charness source checkout to load worktree\n                        helpers from. Defaults to the embedded or managed\n                        checkout.\n```\n\n## `charness worktree doctor`\n\n```text\nusage: charness worktree doctor [-h] [--repo-root REPO_ROOT]\n                                [--require-isolation] [--home-root HOME_ROOT]\n                                [--charness-checkout CHARNESS_CHECKOUT]\n\noptions:\n  -h, --help            show this help message and exit\n  --repo-root REPO_ROOT\n                        Worktree to inspect. Defaults to the current working\n                        directory.\n  --require-isolation   Fail unless this checkout is a linked worktree rather\n                        than the main one. Pass it before handing a WRITE-\n                        CAPABLE agent a checkout: without isolation that agent\n                        shares the parent's tree and index, and a stray git op\n                        lands in the parent's commit. Without the flag,\n                        isolation is reported as a fact and never enforced.\n  --home-root HOME_ROOT\n                        Home root used to locate the managed charness checkout\n                        when the entrypoint is a PATH shim.\n  --charness-checkout CHARNESS_CHECKOUT\n                        Explicit charness source checkout to load worktree\n                        helpers from. Defaults to the embedded or managed\n                        checkout.\n```\n\n## `charness worktree prepare`\n\n```text\nusage: charness worktree prepare [-h] [--repo-root REPO_ROOT] [--force]\n                                 [--no-dependency-reuse]\n                                 [--home-root HOME_ROOT]\n                                 [--charness-checkout CHARNESS_CHECKOUT]\n\noptions:\n  -h, --help            show this help message and exit\n  --repo-root REPO_ROOT\n                        Worktree to prepare. Defaults to the current working\n                        directory.\n  --force               Run prepare even if doctor already reports pass.\n  --no-dependency-reuse\n                        Do not link an installed dependency tree; the declared\n                        install command runs unless doctor coverage skips\n                        prepare (add --force to override that).\n  --home-root HOME_ROOT\n                        Home root used to locate the managed charness checkout\n                        when the entrypoint is a PATH shim.\n  --charness-checkout CHARNESS_CHECKOUT\n                        Explicit charness source checkout to load worktree\n                        helpers from. Defaults to the embedded or managed\n                        checkout.\n```\n\n## `charness worktree audit`\n\n```text\nusage: charness worktree audit [-h] [--repo-root REPO_ROOT]\n                               [--stale-days STALE_DAYS] [--prune] [--doctor]\n                               [--home-root HOME_ROOT]\n                               [--charness-checkout CHARNESS_CHECKOUT]\n\noptions:\n  -h, --help            show this help message and exit\n  --repo-root REPO_ROOT\n                        Repo root whose worktree registry should be audited.\n                        Defaults to the current working directory.\n  --stale-days STALE_DAYS\n                        Detached-HEAD worktrees older than this many days are\n                        reported as stale (default: 14).\n  --prune               After audit, reclaim expired ephemeral worktrees and\n                        prune metadata for missing ones.\n  --doctor              Run readiness doctor for existing worktrees and\n                        include per-worktree readiness summaries.\n  --home-root HOME_ROOT\n                        Home root used to locate the managed charness checkout\n                        when the entrypoint is a PATH shim.\n  --charness-checkout CHARNESS_CHECKOUT\n                        Explicit charness source checkout to load worktree\n                        helpers from. Defaults to the embedded or managed\n                        checkout.\n```\n\n## `charness worktree cleanup`\n\n```text\nusage: charness worktree cleanup [-h] [--repo-root REPO_ROOT] --path PATH\n                                 [--delete-merged-branch]\n                                 [--branch-base BRANCH_BASE] [--yes] [--force]\n                                 [--home-root HOME_ROOT]\n                                 [--charness-checkout CHARNESS_CHECKOUT]\n\noptions:\n  -h, --help            show this help message and exit\n  --repo-root REPO_ROOT\n                        Repo root whose worktree should be operated on.\n                        Defaults to the current working directory.\n  --path PATH           Registered worktree path to remove.\n  --delete-merged-branch\n                        Delete the local branch only after it is contained in\n                        --branch-base.\n  --branch-base BRANCH_BASE\n                        Local ref that must contain the target branch before\n                        branch deletion; defaults to HEAD.\n  --yes                 Execute the planned cleanup. Defaults to dry-run.\n  --force               Pass --force to git worktree remove for dirty targets.\n  --home-root HOME_ROOT\n                        Home root used to locate the managed charness checkout\n                        when the entrypoint is a PATH shim.\n  --charness-checkout CHARNESS_CHECKOUT\n                        Explicit charness source checkout to load worktree\n                        helpers from.     "prompt_content": "\"\"\"Brief critique: the read-only Codex precheck behind prelaunch gates.\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport json\nimport os\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any, Mapping, Sequence\n\n\ndef _load_repo_runtime_bootstrap():\n    pathlib, sys = __import__(\"pathlib\"), __import__(\"sys\")\n    marker = (\"scripts\", \"adapter_lib.py\")\n    parents = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents\n    root = next((p for p in parents if p.joinpath(*marker).is_file()), None)\n    if root is not None and str(root) not in sys.path:\n        sys.path.insert(0, str(root))\n\n\n_load_repo_runtime_bootstrap()\n\nfrom scripts.task_run.task_run_contract import (  # noqa: E402\n    BRIEF_CRITIQUE_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,\n)\n\n\ndef _brief_critique_prompt(\n    brief: str,\n    checks: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],\n    *,\n    lane_executor: str | None = None,\n    lane_executable: str | None = None,\n) -> str:\n    role = \"You are a read-only preflight reviewer for a task lane, not the lane executor. \"\n    if lane_executor:\n        executor_note = f\"The lane itself will run on the {lane_executor!r} executor\"\n        if lane_executable:\n            executor_note += f\", whose executable the task runtime already validated at {lane_executable!r}\"\n        else:\n            executor_note += \", whose availability the task runtime already validated\"\n        executor_note += (\n            \"; your own Codex-only tool context says nothing about that executor's \"\n            \"availability, so never report the lane executor as unavailable because \"\n            \"you lack its tools. \"\n        )\n        role += executor_note\n    return (\n        role\n        + \"Review this task brief read-only against repository code and available provider docs. \"\n        \"Use this fresh context; treat style as advisory. Set premise_failure true only when a central factual premise makes the task \"\n        \"invalid; one blocked item never sets it. Check each premise independently. Return one JSON object with \"\n        \"premise_failure (boolean), premise_failure_reason, findings, and premise_checks \"\n        \"(array of objects with id, kind: success or premise-blocked, and evidence). \"\n        \"Do not edit files.\\n\\nTASK BRIEF:\\n\"\n        + brief\n        + \"\\n\\nPREMISES:\\n\"\n        + json.dumps(list(checks), ensure_ascii=False)\n    )\n\n\ndef _lane_executor_facts(\n    payload: Mapping[str, Any], resolved: Mapping[str, Any]\n) -> tuple[str | None, str | None]:\n    \"\"\"The selected lane carrier and its validated executable, when known.\"\"\"\n    lane_executor = resolved.get(\"executor\")\n    lane_executor = lane_executor if isinstance(lane_executor, str) else None\n    paths = resolved.get(\"executor_paths\")\n    lane_executable = (\n        paths.get(lane_executor)\n        if isinstance(paths, Mapping) and lane_executor is not None\n        else None\n    )\n    if not isinstance(lane_executable, str):\n        block = payload.get(\"executor\")\n        lane_executable = (\n            block.get(\"executable\") if isinstance(block, Mapping) else None\n        )\n    return lane_executor, lane_executable if isinstance(lane_executable, str) else None\n\n\ndef _run_brief_critique(\n    *, payload: dict[str, Any], resolved: Mapping[str, Any], prompt: str,\n    premise_checks: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    from scripts.runtime_bootstrap import import_repo_module\n    from scripts.task_run import task_run_execution as execution\n    from scripts.task_run import task_run_support as support\n    from scripts.task_run.task_run_runtime import (\n        _resolve_codex,\n        build_codex_command,\n        read_only_codex,\n    )\n\n    exec_lib = import_repo_module(__file__, \"scripts.worktree.worktree_exec_lib\")\n\n    executable = _resolve_codex(\"codex\")\n    command = read_only_codex(build_codex_command(executable, effort=\"medium\"))\n    task_root = Path(resolved[\"runtime_path\"]) / \"task-run\" / str(payload[\"task_id\"])\n    stdout_log, stderr_log = task_root / \"brief-critique.stdout.log\", task_root / \"brief-critique.stderr.log\"\n    stdout_log.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    target = Path(resolved[\"target_path\"])\n    execution_root = Path(payload[\"execution_runtime_root\"])\n    configured_env = support.scrubbed_lane_env(\n        payload,\n        exec_lib.prepare_exec_environment(\n            target, os.environ.copy(), runtime_root=execution_root\n        ),\n        \"codex\",\n    )\n    lane_executor, lane_executable = _lane_executor_facts(payload, resolved)\n    outcome = execution._execute_codex(\n        command,\n        prompt=_brief_critique_prompt(\n            prompt,\n            premise_checks,\n            lane_executor=lane_executor,\n            lane_executable=lane_executable,\n        ),\n        target_path=target,\n        configured_env=configured_env,\n        stdout_log=stdout_log,\n        stderr_log=stderr_log,\n        timeout_seconds=BRIEF_CRITIQUE_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,\n    )\n    try:\n        parsed = json.loads(stdout_log.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n    except (OSError, ValueError):\n        parsed = None\n    if outcome.get(\"exit_code\") != 0 or not isinstance(parsed, Mapping):\n        return {\n            \"status\": \"timed-out\" if outcome.get(\"timed_out\") else \"unavailable\",\n            \"error\": str(outcome.get(\"exec_error\") or \"review returned no JSON result\")[:300],\n        }\n    return {\n        \"status\": \"completed\",\n        \"premise_failure\": parsed.get(\"premise_failure\") is True,\n        \"premise_failure_reason\": str(parsed.get(\"premise_failure_reason\") or \"\")[:500],\n        \"findings\": parsed.get(\"findings\"),\n        \"premise_checks\": parsed.get(\"premise_checks\"),\n    }\n",

    "carrier_path": "charness-artifacts/critique/workers/release-8150-final-followup25/semantic-input/0006-content.bin",

    "path": "scripts/task_run/task_run_event_timing.py",
    "prompt_content": "\"\"\"Receive-time stamps on executor event lines, and the command-timing reader.\n\nCodex `--json` items and Muse envelopes carry no wall time (Muse\n`recorded_at` is a logical counter), so a lane's `<executor>.events.log`\ncould not say how long any command or phase took. The carrier now reads the\nexecutor's stdout through a pipe and inserts `charness_received_at` (UTC,\nmillisecond ISO-8601) at the front of every JSON-object line as it arrives.\nNon-JSON lines pass through unchanged. The stamp is the time charness\nreceived the line, not the time the executor produced it; pipe buffering\nmakes it an upper bound on the producer's own time, so a duration between\ntwo stamps can err in either direction.\n\n`command_timing` reads a stamped events log back into per-command durations\n(`item.started` to `item.completed` for Codex, `task.lifecycle.started` to\n`task.lifecycle.completed` of a `tool.bash` task for Muse), starts per\ncommand family, and the first time each lane phase marker was seen.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport json\nimport os\nimport re\nimport select\nimport threading\nimport time\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\nfrom typing import Any, BinaryIO, Callable, Iterable, Mapping\n\nRECEIVED_AT_FIELD = \"charness_received_at\"\n_FIELD_BYTES = json.dumps(RECEIVED_AT_FIELD).encode(\"ascii\")\n_CLOSE_GRACE_SECONDS = 2.0\n_POLL_SECONDS = 0.1\n_CHUNK_BYTES = 64 * 1024\n_LANE_PHASES = (\"CONTRACT-READ\", \"EDITING\", \"TESTING\")\n\n#: Runner invocations, not mentions: `gateway/test/x.ts` is not a test run.\n#: A heuristic over command text; one command may match several families\n#: (`pnpm run lint && pnpm run check:types` counts in both).\n_PM = r\"\\b(?:pnpm|npm|yarn|bun)\\b[^;&|\\n]*?\\s(?:run\\s+)?\"\nCOMMAND_FAMILIES: tuple[tuple[str, re.Pattern[str]], ...] = (\n    (\n        \"test\",\n        re.compile(\n            _PM + r\"test(?::[\\w-]+)?\\b|\\bpytest\\b|\\bvitest\\b|\\bjest\\b\"\n            r\"|\\b(?:go|cargo)\\s+test\\b|node\\s+--test\\b|run_standing_pytest\"\n        ),\n    ),\n    (\n        \"lint\",\n        re.compile(\n            _PM + r\"lint(?::[\\w-]+)?\\b|\\beslint\\b|\\bruff\\s+(?:check|format)\\b\"\n            r\"|\\bgolangci-lint\\b|\\bgofmt\\b|check-docs\\.sh\"\n        ),\n    ),\n    (\n        \"typecheck\",\n        re.compile(\n            _PM + r\"(?:check:types|typecheck)\\b|\\btsc\\b|\\bmypy\\b|\\bpyright\\b|\\bgo\\s+vet\\b\"\n        ),\n    ),\n    (\"merge\", re.compile(r\"\\bgit\\s+(?:merge|rebase|cherry-pick)\\b\")),\n)\n\n\ndef utc_receive_stamp() -> str:\n    \"\"\"The production receive clock: UTC wall time to the millisecond.\"\"\"\n    return (\n        datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec=\"milliseconds\").replace(\"+00:00\", \"Z\")\n    )\n\n\ndef stamp_event_line(line: bytes, received_at: str) -> bytes:\n    \"\"\"Insert the receive-time field first in one JSON-object line.\n\n    The rest of the line keeps its original bytes, so a reader that ignores\n    the field sees the executor's event unchanged.\n    \"\"\"\n    start = line.find(b\"{\")\n    if start < 0 or line[:start].strip():\n        return line\n    try:\n        event = json.loads(line)\n    except ValueError:\n        return line\n    if not isinstance(event, dict) or RECEIVED_AT_FIELD in event:\n        return line\n    head, rest = line[: start + 1], line[start + 1 :]\n    separator = b\"\" if rest.lstrip().startswith(b\"}\") else b\",\"\n    stamp = _FIELD_BYTES + b\":\" + json.dumps(received_at).encode(\"ascii\")\n    return head + stamp + separator + rest\n\n\nclass StampingPipe:\n    \"\"\"A pipe whose reader copies each line into `sink` with its receive time.\n\n    `fileno()` is the write end, so the carrier can hand it to the executor\n    as stdout. An optional `observer` sees each complete JSON event after its\n    bytes reach the sink (the lane progress rule reads commands from it).\n    `close()` releases the write end, then drains the bytes already in the\n    pipe and returns; it never waits on a writer that is still alive. Unless\n    a loss case below occurs, bytes are retained byte-for-byte up to the\n    moment of close: once the executor's process group is killed, everything\n    it wrote is in the kernel pipe buffer, so the drain reads it all.\n\n    Five things can still lose bytes, and none is silent: an observer that\n    holds the reader past the close grace (the progress rule runs worktree\n    probes on it; recorded as `writer_open_at_close` or a reader error), a descendant\n    outside the killed group that keeps the pipe open (its later output is\n    not captured, `writer_open_at_close`), a pipe read that fails (`error`;\n    the reader stops and bytes still queued are not drained), a sink write that fails or is\n    short (`error`; the reader keeps reading and counts `dropped_bytes`, so\n    the executor does not block on a full pipe), and a sink write that hangs.\n    A hung write holds the reader, so the pipe stops draining; `close()`\n    waits for it only a bounded time, then returns with `error` set while\n    that write may still be in flight. Every later write is dropped, never\n    sent to a sink the caller closed, but the in-flight one is the sink's\n    own: a caller that closes a hung sink closes it under that write.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        sink: BinaryIO,\n        *,\n        clock: Callable[[], str] = utc_receive_stamp,\n        observer: Callable[[Mapping[str, Any]], None] | None = None,\n    ) -> None:\n        self._sink = sink\n        self._clock = clock\n        self._observer = observer\n        self._read_fd, self._write_fd = os.pipe()\n        self._stop = threading.Event()\n        self._sink_lock = threading.Lock()\n        self._released = False\n        self._sink_failed = False\n        self._pending = b\"\"\n        self.received_bytes = 0\n        self.dropped_bytes = 0\n        self.error: str | None = None\n        self.writer_open_at_close = False\n        self._thread = threading.Thread(\n            target=self._drain, name=\"charness-event-stamp\", daemon=True\n        )\n        self._thread.start()\n\n    def fileno(self) -> int:\n        return self._write_fd\n\n    def _emit(self, data: bytes) -> None:\n        with self._sink_lock:\n            if self._released:\n                self.dropped_bytes += len(data)\n                return\n            if not self._sink_failed:\n                try:\n                    written = self._sink.write(data)\n                    if isinstance(written, int) and written < len(data):\n                        # A sink that accepted part of the line: record the rest as lost.\n                        self._sink_failed = True\n                        self.error = self.error or f\"short write: {written} of {len(data)} bytes\"\n                        self.dropped_bytes += len(data) - written\n                        return\n                    self._sink.flush()\n                    return\n                except (OSError, ValueError) as exc:\n                    self._sink_failed = True\n                    self.error = self.error or f\"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}\"\n            self.dropped_bytes += len(data)\n\n    def _stamped(self, line: bytes) -> bytes:\n        try:\n            return stamp_event_line(line, self._clock())\n        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - keep the bytes, record the clock fault\n            self.error = self.error or f\"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}\"\n            return line\n\n    def _feed(self, chunk: bytes) -> None:\n        self.received_bytes += len(chunk)\n        *lines, self._pending = (self._pending + chunk).split(b\"\\n\")\n        for line in lines:\n            self._emit(self._stamped(line + b\"\\n\"))\n            self._observe(line)\n\n    def _observe(self, line: bytes) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Hand each complete JSON-object event to the observer, after it is kept.\"\"\"\n        if self._observer is None or not line.lstrip().startswith(b\"{\"):\n            return\n        try:\n            event = json.loads(line)\n        except ValueError:\n            return\n        if isinstance(event, dict):\n            try:\n                self._observer(event)\n            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - an observer fault must not cost event bytes\n                pass\n\n    def _drain(self) -> None:\n        deadline: float | None = None\n        try:\n            while True:\n                timeout = _POLL_SECONDS\n                if self._stop.is_set():\n                    # Closing: keep reading what is buffered, and give a just\n                    # killed writer a short grace to release its end; never\n                    # wait on a writer that outlives it.\n                    deadline = deadline or time.monotonic() + _CLOSE_GRACE_SECONDS\n                    timeout = deadline - time.monotonic()\n                    if timeout <= 0:\n                        self.writer_open_at_close = True\n                        break\n                readable, _, _ = select.select([self._read_fd], [], [], timeout)\n                if not readable:\n                    continue\n                chunk = os.read(self._read_fd, _CHUNK_BYTES)\n                if not chunk:\n                    break\n                self._feed(chunk)\n        except (OSError, ValueError) as exc:\n            self.error = self.error or f\"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}\"\n        finally:\n            if self._pending:\n                self._emit(self._stamped(self._pending))\n                self._pending = b\"\"\n            os.close(self._read_fd)\n\n    def close(self) -> None:\n        if self._write_fd >= 0:\n            os.close(self._write_fd)\n            self._write_fd = -1\n        self._stop.set()\n        self._thread.join(timeout=_CLOSE_GRACE_SECONDS + 1.0)\n        # The caller closes the sink after this; a reader still running (a\n        # hung sink write holds the lock) must drop, not write into a closed\n        # file. The lock wait is bounded so a hung sink cannot hang close.\n        locked = self._sink_lock.acquire(timeout=_CLOSE_GRACE_SECONDS)\n        try:\n            self._released = True\n            if self._thread.is_alive():\n                self.error = self.error or \"event reader did not finish before close\"\n        finally:\n            if locked:\n                self._sink_lock.release()\n\n    def capture(self) -> dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"The capture receipt: complete only if every received byte reached the sink.\"\"\"\n        return {\n            \"complete\": not (self.error or self.writer_open_at_close or self.dropped_bytes),\n            \"received_bytes\": self.received_bytes,\n            \"dropped_bytes\": self.dropped_bytes,\n            \"writer_open_at_close\": self.writer_open_at_close,\n            \"error\": self.error,\n        }\n\n\ndef _parse_stamp(value: object) -> datetime | None:\n    if not isinstance(value, str):\n        return None\n    try:\n        parsed = datetime.fromisoformat(value.replace(\"Z\", \"+00:00\"))\n    except ValueError:\n        return None\n    # A naive value cannot be ordered against the carrier's UTC stamps, so it\n    # counts as unstamped rather than crashing the subtraction later.\n    return parsed if parsed.utcoffset() is not None else None\n\n\ndef _stamped_events(text: str) -> Iterable[tuple[datetime | None, dict[str, Any]]]:\n    for line in text.splitlines():\n        if not line.lstrip().startswith(\"{\"):\n            continue\n        try:\n            event = json.loads(line)\n        except ValueError:\n            continue\n        if isinstance(event, dict):\n            yield _parse_stamp(event.get(RECEIVED_AT_FIELD)), event\n\n\ndef command_families(command: str) -> list[str]:\n    return [name for name, pattern in COMMAND_FAMILIES if pattern.search(command)]\n\n\ndef _muse_command(payload: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str | None:\n Compliant executors exit promptly on their own; only a lane that\n#: lingers past the grace is killed, so a block declaration cannot become a\n#: full-timeout consumption.\nDEFAULT_BLOCKED_GRACE_SECONDS = 60.0\n#: Consecutive failed progress checks before the watch stops the lane: with\n#: no wall clock, a watch that cannot check must not leave a lane unbounded.\nMAX_RULE_FAILURES = 8\n\n\nclass LaneProgressWatch:\n    \"\"\"Live phase relay and progress guards for one lane.\n\n    Every watched lane runs the progress rule (`task_run_progress_rule`); a\n    require-change lane also gets the first-edit budget and BLOCKED grace.\n\n    The carrier cannot stream the executor's own pipes (they are redirected\n    into the lane logs), so the watch tails those logs on a poll cadence and\n    relays phase changes as `PROGRESS` lines on the carrier's own stderr —\n    the parent observes \"alive and editing\" versus \"alive but exploring\"\n    without tailing private executor logs (#815).    self._action_lock = threading.Lock()\n        self._preempted = False\n        self.preemptions = 0\n        self._rule_failures = 0\n        self.suppressed_actions = 0\n        self._undelivered: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n\n    def start(\n        self,\n        *,\n        emit: Callable[[list[str], float], None],\n        kill: Callable[[], None],\n    ) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Begin polling beside the running lane; `kill` stops its process group.\"\"\"\n        self._emit = emit\n        self._kill = kill\n        self._started_at = self._clock()\n        if self._progress_rule name=\"charness-lane-progress\", daemon=True\n        )\n        self._thread.start()\n\n    def stop(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Halt polling; safe to call when the lane already exited.\"\"\"\n \"blocked_grace_seconds\": self._blocked_grace_seconds,\n            \"poll_seconds\": self._poll_seconds,\n            \"stop_reason\": self.stop_reason,\n            \"preemptions\":           return None\n        checks_before = len(self._progress_rule.checks)\n        try:\n            action = self._progress_rule.on_tick(now)\n        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - counted; repeated failure stops the lane\n            self._rule_failures += 1\n            if self._rule_failures < MAX_RULE_FAILURES:\n                return None\n            return (\n self._progress_rule.finish(ended_normally=ended_normally)\n\n    def observe_event(self, event: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Feed one executor event to the progress rule (runs on the event reader).\"\"\"\n        if self._progress_rule is None or self.stop_reason is not None:\n            return\n        action = self._progress_rule.on_event(event)\n        if action is not None \"\"\"Deliver the rule's message now: queue it as a steer, then preempt.\n\n        The kill ends this executor invocation; the execution loop sees the\n        preemption and resumes the same session with self._action_lock:\n            # First wins: once the lane is stopping (here or in the rule), a\n            # message would never be delivered, so it is suppressed and counted.\n            rule = self._progress_rule\n            if self.stop_reason is not None or (rule is not None and rule.stop_reason):\n                self.suppressed_actions += 1\n                return\n            self._undelivered.append(\n                _lane_runner.enqueue_steer(\n                    self._steer_queue,\n                    self._stdout_log.parent.name,\n                    action.text,\n                    reason=action.reason_code,\n                    actor=\"charness-progress-rule\",\n                )\n            )\n            if not self._preempted:\n                # One preemption per invocation; later messages ride along.\n                self._preempted = True\n                self.preemptions += 1\n                self._kill()\n\n    def take_preempted(self) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Whether the last invocation ended because the watch preempted it.\"\"\"\n        preempted, self._preempted = self._preempted, False\n        return preempted\n\n    def _stop_now(self, reason: str) -> None:\n        from scripts.task_run import task_run_lane_runner as _lane_runner\n\n        with self._action_lock:\n            if self.stop_reason is not None:\n                return\n            self.stop_reason = reason\n            # A message queued in this invocation will never be delivered: the\n            # stop supersedes it, and its preemption did not \"nacked\", \"reason_code\": \"superseded-by-stop\"},\n                )\n                self.suppressed_actions += 1\n            self._undelivered = []\n            if self._preempted:\n                self._preempted = False\n                self.preemptions -= 1\n        try:\n            self.fire(reason)\n        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - a failed stop must not kill the reader\n            pass\n\n    def pending_steers(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:\n        \"\"\"Read the queue once more at the turn boundary and return undelivered entries.\"\"\"\n        self._poll_steer_queue()\n        return [message for message in self._steer_messages if message.get(\"delivered_at\") is None]\n\n    def record_steer_delivery(\n        self, messages: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]], *, resume_path: str\n    ) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Record the typed delivery disposition after the next invocation starts.\"\"\"\n        by_id = {item.get(\"message_id\"): item for item in self._steer_messages}\n        delivered = {message.get(\"message_id\") for message in messages}\n        with self._action_lock:\n            self._undelivered = [\n                item for item in self._undelivered if item.get(\"message_id\") not in delivered\n            ]\n        stamp = _support.utc_now_iso()\n        from scripts.task_run import task_run_lane_runner as _lane_runner\n\n        for message in messages:\n            current = by_id.get(message.get(\"message_id\"))\n            if current is not None:\n                current.update(\n                    delivered_at=stamp,\n                    disposition=\"accepted\",\n                    reason=None,\n                    resume_path=resume_path,\n                )\n                _lane_runner.update_steer_queue(self._steer_queue, current)\n\n    def _poll_steer_queue(self) -> None:\n        from scripts.task_run import task_run_lane_runner as _lane_runner\n\n        for message in _lane_runner.read_steer_queue(self._steer_queue):\n            message_id = message.get(\"message_id\")\n            if not isinstance(message_id, str) or
