lane-branch commit count, and the last\n        commit subject. Tool-call counts are executor-private and stay out;\n        log silence is reported as seconds since the logs last grew.\n        \"\"\"\n        lively = _worktree_liveliness(self._worktree, self._base_sha)\n        lively = lively if isinstance(lively, dict) else {}\n        age = None if self._last_growth_at is None else max(0.0, now - self._last_growth_at)\n        progress = None if self._progress_rule is None else self._progress_rule.snapshot(now)\n        return {\n            \"progress_rule\": progress,\n            \"phase\": self._last_phases[-1] if self._last_phases else None,\n            \"phases\": list(self._last_phases),\n            \"files_changed\": lively.get(\"files_changed\"),\n            \"commits\": lively.get(\"commits\"),\n            \"last_commit_subject\": lively.get(\"last_commit_subject\"),\n            \"seconds_since_log_growth\": age,\n        }\n\n    def _track_log_growth(self, now: float) -> None:\n        try:\n            sizes = (\n                self._stdout_log.stat().st_size if self._stdout_log.is_file() else 0,\n                self._stderr_log.stat().st_size if self._stderr_log.is_file() else 0,\n            )\n        except OSError:\n            return\n        if sizes != self._last_log_sizes:\n            self._last_log_sizes = sizes\n            self._last_growth_at = now\n\n    def _poll(self, now: float) -> str | None:\n        \"\"\"One poll: relay new phases and report a stop reason, if due.\n\n        Cumulative: markers are one-line events the executor need not\n        repeat, so phases union into the watched set and the first\n        `CONTRACT-READ` observation time is kept. A marker that aged out of\n        the tail window still counts (#815).\n        \"\"\"\n        progress = lane_progress(\n            _evidence._tail_text(self._stdout_log),\n            _evidence._tail_text(self._stderr_log),\n        )\n        fresh = [phase for phase in progress[\"phases\"]]\n        new = [phase for phase in LANE_PHASES if phase in fresh and phase not in self._last_phases]\n        if new:\n            self._last_phases = [\n                phase\n                for phase in LANE_PHASES\n                if phase in self._last_phases or phase in fresh\n            ]\n            if self._emit is not None and self._started_at is not None:\n                self._emit(list(self._last_phases), now - self._started_at)\n        phases = list(self._last_phases)\n        # The declared blocker is cumulative like phases: a marker that ages\n        # out of the tail window still counts, so a lingering lane cannot\n        # evade the grace by emitting enough output after declaring BLOCKED.\n        observed = progress[\"blocker\"]\n        if observed is not None and self._blocker is None:\n            self._blocker = observed\n        blocker = observed if observed is not None else self._blocker\n        if blocker is not None:\n            # A declared block is terminal semantics, not a stall to watch:\n            # a lane that lingers past the grace after declaring BLOCKED is\n            # stopped, so the declaration cannot burn the full timeout. A\n            # non-positive grace turns the linger stop off independently of\n            # the no-progress budget.\n            if self._blocked_grace_seconds <= 0:\n                return None\n            if self._blocker_at is None:\n                self._blocker_at = now\n                return None\n            if now - self._blocker_at < self._blocked_grace_seconds:\n                return None\n            return (\n                f\"lane declared BLOCKED ({blocker}) but kept running; \"\n                f\"stopped after {self._blocked_grace_seconds:g}s\"\n            )\n        if \"CONTRACT-READ\" not in phases:\n            return None\n        if self._contract_read_at is None:\n            self._contract_read_at = now\n        diff_present = scoped_diff_present(\n            self._worktree, self._base_sha, self._scope_specs\n        )\n        if diff_present:\n            self._maybe_snapshot_scoped_diff(now)\n        return no_progress_stop_due(\n            phases=phases,\n            contract_read_at=self._contract_read_at,\n            now=now,\n            budget_seconds=self._budget_seconds,\n            diff_present=diff_present,\n        )\n\n    def _maybe_snapshot_scoped_diff(self, now: float) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Keep the first observed scoped diff so a self-revert stays recoverable (#834).\n\n        Best-effort and once-only: the guard must never fail a lane it is\n        watching, so any unobservable worktree simply leaves the snapshot\n        empty for the receipt backstop to report as unobserved.\n        \"\"\"\n        if self._first_scoped_paths is not None:\n            return\n        snapshot = _signals.scoped_diff_snapshot(self._worktree, self._base_sha, self._scope_specs)\n        if snapshot is None:\n            return\n        self._first_scoped_paths, self._first_scoped_diff, self._first_scoped_truncated = snapshot\n        if self._started_at is not None:\n            self._first_scoped_elapsed = max(0.0, now - self._started_at)\n\n    def fire(self, reason: str) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Record the typed stop on the transcript and kill the stalled lane.\"\"\"\n        try:\n            with self._stderr_log.open(\"a\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as handle:\n                handle.write(f\"{NO_PROGRESS_STOP_PREFIX} {reason}\\n\")\n        except OSError:\n            pass\n        finally:\n            if self._kill is not None:\n                self._kill()\n\n    def _run(self) -> None:\n        while not self._stopped.wait(self._poll_seconds):\n            if self.stop_reason is not None:\n                return\n            try:\n                reason = self.tick(self._clock())\n            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - a failed poll must not kill the watch\n                continue\n            if reason is None:\n                continue\n            self._stop_now(reason)\n            return\n\n\ndef build_progress_watch(\n    *,\n    require_change: bool,\n    stdout_log: Path,\n    stderr_log: Path,\n    worktree: Path,\n    base_sha: str,\n    scope_specs: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],\n    observe: Callable[[dict[str, Any]], None] | None = None,\n    budget_override: float | None = None,\n) -> LaneProgressWatch | None:\n    \"\"\"The live watch for a lane, or None when nothing would be watched.\n\n    Every lane gets the progress rule (`task_run_progress_rule`), the one\n    stop a lane has once no wall clock bounds it; `CHARNESS_TASK_RUN_PROGRESS_WINDOW_SECONDS`\n    sets its window and `0` turns it off. A require-change lane also gets the\n    first-edit budget and the BLOCKED linger grace. The guard owns queue\n    polling for watched lanes. Execution checks the same queue at every\n    natural executor boundary when this guard is absent. An explicit\n    ``budget_override`` (``--no-progress-seconds``) wins over the environment\n    so a contract-heavy lane records its budget as a deliberate per-lane\n    choice (#835).\n    \"\"\"\n    window_seconds, window_parsed = _env_seconds(\n        _rule.PROGRESS_WINDOW_ENV, _rule.DEFAULT_PROGRESS_WINDOW_SECONDS\n    )\n    if window_seconds < 0:  # only an explicit 0 turns off a lane's one bound\n        window_seconds, window_parsed = _rule.DEFAULT_PROGRESS_WINDOW_SECONDS, False\n    if not require_change and window_seconds <= 0:\n        return None\n    progress_rule = _rule.ProgressRule(\n        window_seconds=window_seconds,\n        fingerprint=lambda: _rule.worktree_fingerprint(worktree),\n        window_source=\"env\" if window_parsed else \"default\",\n    )\n    budget_raw = os.environ.get(NO_PROGRESS_BUDGET_ENV)\n    if not require_change:\n        budget_seconds, budget_source = 0.0, \"disabled\"\n    elif budget_override is None:\n        budget_seconds, budget_parsed = _env_seconds(\n            NO_PROGRESS_BUDGET_ENV, DEFAULT_NO_PROGRESS_BUDGET_SECONDS\n        )\n        budget_source = \"env\" if budget_parsed else \"default\"\n    else:\n        budget_seconds = budget_override\n        budget_source = \"flag\"\n    poll_seconds, _ = _env_seconds(PROGRESS_POLL_ENV, DEFAULT_PROGRESS_POLL_SECONDS)\n    grace_seconds, _ = _env_seconds(BLOCKED_GRACE_ENV, DEFAULT_BLOCKED_GRACE_SECONDS)\n    return LaneProgressWatch(\n        stdout_log=stdout_log,\n        stderr_log=stderr_log,\n        worktree=worktree,\n        base_sha=base_sha,\n        scope_specs=scope_specs,\n        budget_seconds=budget_seconds,\n        poll_seconds=poll_seconds,\n        blocked_grace_seconds=grace_seconds if require_change else 0.0,\n        observe=observe,\n        budget_source=budget_source,\n        budget_raw=budget_raw,\n        progress_rule=progress_rule,\n    )\n\n\ndef _execute_watched_lane(payload: dict[str, Any], command: list[str], **kwargs: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Run the lane executor; the attempt loop owns retries (see task_run_attempts).\"\"\"\n    from scripts.task_run import task_run_attempts as _attempts\n\n    return _attempts._execute_watched_lane(payload, command, **kwargs)\n",

    "prompt_content": "\"\"\"The lane progress rule: the one stop condition a task-run lane has.\n\nA wall-clock timeout killed productive lanes mid-edit and let a looping lane\nburn its whole budget. This rule replaces it with progress. Progress since the\nlast check is any of: a new commit, a file change, or a new command outcome\n(a completed command whose (command text, worktree fingerprint) pair this lane\nhas not completed before; rerunning a command on an unchanged tree is not\nnew). Every progress window the rule checks. A window without progress earns\na checkpoint request; a second one in a row, with the request unanswered,\nstops the lane. A command that started since the previous check and is still\nrunning defers the request, and so the stop, by one check. Windows are\nnominal: the watch runs a due check at its next poll. A hung command, a silent model and a dead executor are the\nsame case, so there is no second timer.\n\nLoops are judged at each command completion: the third completion of the\nsame test, lint or typecheck command on an unchanged worktree asks the lane\nto switch to the debug skill, the fourth asks it to checkpoint and stop, and\nthe fifth stops it.\n\nThe rule is pure over its inputs: the clock is the caller's `now`, the\nworktree is read through an injected fingerprint. An unobservable worktree\n(`None`) shows neither progress nor its absence: the window check falls back\nto the executor's own events, so a silent lane is still stopped, and no loop\nis claimed without seeing the tree. The watch (`task_run_progress`) applies its actions.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport hashlib\nimport os\nimport threading\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any, Callable, Mapping\n\n\ndef _load_repo_runtime_bootstrap():\n    pathlib, sys = __import__(\"pathlib\"), __import__(\"sys\")\n    marker = (\"scripts\", \"adapter_lib.py\")\n    parents = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents\n    root = next((p for p in parents if p.joinpath(*marker).is_file()), None)\n    if root is not None and str(root) not in sys.path:\n        sys.path.insert(0, str(root))\n\n\n_load_repo_runtime_bootstrap()\n\nfrom scripts.core.subprocess_guard import run_process  # noqa: E402\nfrom scripts.task_run import task_run_event_timing as _timing  # noqa: E402\n\n#: Muse task kinds that change files (seen in ceal lane logs, 2026-10-20);\n#: reads, searches, todo lists and bash input are activity, not outcomes.\nMUSE_EDIT_TOOL_KINDS = frozenset({\"tool.edit\", \"tool.edit_file\", \"tool.write_file\"})\nPROGRESS_WINDOW_ENV = \"CHARNESS_TASK_RUN_PROGRESS_WINDOW_SECONDS\"\nDEFAULT_PROGRESS_WINDOW_SECONDS = 1200.0\nLOOP_FAMILIES = frozenset({\"test\", \"lint\", \"typecheck\"})\n_FINGERPRINT_PROBE_SECONDS = 60.0\n\nCHECKPOINT_REQUEST = (\n    \"charness progress check: no commit, file change, or new command result in the \"\n    \"last {window}. If your work has a coherent state, commit it now (hooks must \"\n    \"pass; never --no-verify) and write your final report naming what remains. If \"\n    \"you are blocked, reply on its own line: BLOCKED: <reason>. If you are still \"\n    \"making progress, continue; charness stops this lane if the next {window} also \"\n    \"shows no commit, file change, or new command result.\"\n)\nLOOP_DEBUG_REQUEST = (\n    \"charness loop check: `{command}` has now completed {count} times with no file \"\n    \"change between runs{exit}. Running it again will not change the result. Switch \"\n    \"to the debug skill: read the failure output, state one falsifiable hypothesis, \"\n    \"and change code, test, or approach before you run it again.\"\n)\nLOOP_CHECKPOINT_REQUEST = (\n    \"charness loop check: `{command}` ran a {count}th time on an unchanged worktree. \"\n    \"Checkpoint now: commit the coherent part of your work if there is one (hooks \"\n    \"must pass; never --no-verify), write your final report naming what is \"\n    \"unresolved, and stop. One more identical run stops this lane.\"\n)\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass Action:\n    \"\"\"What the watch must do now: send `message` (preempting) or `stop`.\"\"\"\n\n    kind: str\n    text: str\n    reason_code: str\n\n\ndef worktree_fingerprint(worktree: Path) -> str | None:\n    \"\"\"One hash of HEAD, the tracked diff, and untracked files, or None if unobservable.\n\n    Ignored files (build output, caches) do not count. Untracked files are\n    read by size and modification time, not content, so a rewrite counts as a\n    change; the error is toward progress, never toward a false stop.\n    \"\"\"\n    digest = hashlib.sha256()\n    for args in (\n        (\"rev-parse\", \"HEAD\"),\n        (\"diff\", \"HEAD\", \"--no-ext-diff\", \"--no-color\", \"--binary\"),\n        (\"ls-files\", \"--others\", \"--exclude-standard\", \"-z\"),\n    ):\n        try:\n            result = run_process(\n                [\"git\", *args], cwd=worktree, timeout_seconds=_FINGERPRINT_PROBE_SECONDS\n            )\n        except OSError:\n            return None\n        if result.returncode != 0:\n            return None\n        digest.update(result.stdout.encode(\"utf-8\", \"surrogateescape\") + b\"\\0\")\n        if args[0] == \"ls-files\":\n            for name in sorted(filter(None, result.stdout.split(\"\\0\"))):\n                try:\n                    stat = os.stat(worktree / name, follow_symlinks=False)\n                except OSError:\n                    continue\n                digest.update(f\"{name}\\0{stat.st_size}\\0{stat.st_mtime_ns}\\0\".encode(\n                    \"utf-8\", \"surrogateescape\"\n                ))\n    return digest.hexdigest()\n\n\ndef _duration(seconds: float) -> str:\n    return f\"{seconds / 60:g} minutes\" if seconds >= 60 else f\"{seconds:g} seconds\"\n\n\nclass ProgressRule:\n    \"\"\"Window checks and loop detection for one lane; thread-safe.\n\n    `on_event` runs on the event reader as each executor line arrives;\n    `on_tick` runs on the watch's poll. Both return an `Action` or None.\n    Request states: None, `deferred` (a command was in flight at an empty\n    check; delivery waits for its completion or the next check),\n    `delivered-between-checks` (delivered at a command completion; the next\n    check starts the request's full window instead of stopping), and\n    `delivered` (an empty check stops the lane, unless a command younger\n    than one window is running, which defers the stop a check).\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        *,\n        window_seconds: float,\n        fingerprint: Callable[[], str | None],\n        window_source: str = \"default\",\n    ) -> None:\n        self.window_seconds = window_seconds\n        self.window_source = window_source\n        self._fingerprint = fingerprint\n        self._lock = threading.Lock()\n        self._timeline = _timing._Timeline(on_complete=self._on_complete)\n        self._muse_kinds: dict[str, str] = {}\n        self._outcomes: dict[tuple[str, str], int] = {}\n        self._pending: Action | None = None\n        self._last_check_at: float | None = None\n        self._last_state: str | None = None\n        self._last_progress_at: float | None = None\n        self._new_outcomes = 0\n        self._request: str | None = None\n        self._in_flight_at_check: set[str] = set()\n        self._invocation = 0\n        self.checks: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n        self.messages: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n        self.stop_reason: str | None = None\n\n    def start(self, now: float) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Begin observing at `now`; a later call starts the next invocation.\n\n        Window state carries over a preemption; command state does not: the\n        previous executor process is dead, and a resumed Codex session reuses\n        item ids, so each invocation gets a fresh timeline and its own keys.\n        \"\"\"\n        with self._lock:\n            if self._last_check_at is None:\n                self._last_check_at = now\n                self._last_progress_at = now\n                self._last_state = self._fingerprint()\n            else:\n                self._invocation += 1\n                self._timeline = _timing._Timeline(on_complete=self._on_complete)\n                self._muse_kinds = {}\n\n    def _in_flight(self) -> dict[str, str]:\n        return {\n            f\"{self._invocation}:{key}\": command\n            for key, command in self._timeline.in_flight().items()\n        }\n\n    def on_event(self, event: Mapping[str, Any]) -> Action | None:\n        with self._lock:\n            if self.stop_reason is not None or self.window_seconds <= 0:\n                return None  # stopped, or the rule is off: no message reaches the lane\n            self._count_edit_outcome(event)\n            self._timeline.feed(None, event)\n            action, self._pending = self._pending, None\n            return self._record(action)\n\n    def _count_edit_outcome(self, event: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None:\n        \"\"\"File edits the executor reports are outcomes even when git cannot be read.\"\"\"\n        item = event.get(\"item\")\n        if (\n            event.get(\"type\") == \"item.completed\"\n            and isinstance(item, Mapping)\n            and item.get(\"type\") == \"file_change\"\n        ):\n            self._new_outcomes += 1\n            return\n        payload = event.get(\"payload\")\n        if not isinstance(payload, Mapping):\n            return\n        task_id = str(payload.get(\"task_id\"))\n        inner = payload.get(\"event\")\n        if event.get(\"payload_type\") == \"task.lifecycle.proposed\" and isinstance(inner, Mapping):\n            self._muse_kinds[task_id] = str(inner.get(\"task_kind\") or \"\")\n        elif event.get(\"payload_type\") == \"task.lifecycle.completed\":\n            if self._muse_kinds.get(task_id, \"\") in MUSE_EDIT_TOOL_KINDS:\n                self._new_outcomes += 1\n\n    def _on_complete(self, command: str | None, exit_code: object) -> None:\n        if not command:\n            self._new_outcomes += 1  # an unnamed command cannot be proven a repeat\n            return\n        state = self._fingerprint()\n        # An unreadable tree is one unknown state: a new command is still new,\n        # a rerun is not, and no loop is claimed without seeing the tree.\n        key = (command.strip(), state or \"unobservable\")\n        count = self._outcomes.get(key, 0) + 1\n        self._outcomes[key] = count\n        if count == 1:\n            self._new_outcomes += 1\n        if state is None:\n            return\n        if count > 1 and self._request == \"deferred\" and not self._in_flight():\n            # The deferred request waited for this boundary, and the command\n            # produced nothing new: deliver it now. It arrives between checks,\n            # so the next check opens its full window rather than stopping.\n            self._request = \"delivered-between-checks\"\n            self._pending = self._checkpoint_request(interrupted=[])\n            return\n        if count < 3 or not LOOP_FAMILIES & set(_timing.command_families(command)):\n            return\n        shown = command.strip()[:200]\n        if count == 3:\n            ending = f\" (last exit code {exit_code})\" if isinstance(exit_code, int) else \"\"\n            self._pending = Action(\n                \"message\",\n                LOOP_DEBUG_REQUEST.format(command=shown, count=count, exit=ending),\n                \"loop-debug\",\n            )\n        elif count == 4:\n            self._pending = Action(\n                \"message\",\n                LOOP_CHECKPOINT_REQUEST.format(command=shown, count=count),\n                \"loop-checkpoint\",\n            )\n        elif count == 5:\n            self._pending = Action(\n                \"stop\",\n                f\"loop: `{shown}` completed {count} times on an unchanged worktree; \"\n                \"the debug and checkpoint requests went unanswered\",\n                \"loop-stop\",\n            )\n\n    def _checkpoint_request(self, *, interrupted: list[str]) -> Action:\n        text = CHECKPOINT_REQUEST.format(window=_duration(self.window_seconds))\n        if interrupted:\n            # The kill takes the executor's whole process group, so every\n            # running command is interrupted, not only the oldest one.\n            named = \", \".join(f\"`{command[:200]}`\" for command in interrupted)\n            text += (\n                f\" Every command still running was interrupted ({named}), each after \"\n                f\"running for at least {_duration(self.window_seconds)}; they may have \"\n                \"left partial output or files. Do not simply rerun them: run a narrower \"\n                \"or faster variant, or report that one needs longer.\"\n            )\n        return Action(\"message\", text, \"no-progress\")\n\n    def on_tick(self, now: float) -> Action | None:\n        with self._lock:\n            if self.window_seconds <= 0 or self._last_check_at is None:\n                return None\n            if now - self._last_check_at < self.window_seconds:\n                return None\n            state = self._fingerprint()\n            tree_changed = state is not None and state != self._last_state\n            progressed = tree_changed or self._new_outcomes > 0\n            in_flight = self._in_flight()\n            young = set(in_flight) - self._in_flight_at_check\n            self._in_flight_at_check = set(in_flight)\n            check = {\n                \"elapsed_s\": round(now - self._last_check_at, 3),\n                \"progress\": progressed,\n                \"new_outcomes\": self._new_outcomes,\n                \"tree_changed\": tree_changed,\n                \"tree_observable\": state is not None,\n                \"commands_in_flight\": len(in_flight),\n                \"commands_started_since_check\": len(young),\n                \"request_before\": self._request,\n            }\n            self.checks.append(check)\n            self._last_check_at = now\n            self._last_state = state if state is not None else self._last_state\n            self._new_outcomes = 0\n            if progressed:\n                self._last_progress_at = now\n                if self._request in (\"delivered\", \"delivered-between-checks\"):\n                    self._mark_answered(True, \"progress\")\n                self._request = None\n                return None\n            if young:\n                # A command started after the last check has run less than one\n                # window, and preemption never kills a command that young: the\n                # request (or stop) waits for its completion or the next check.\n                check[\"deferred_for_young_command\"] = True\n                if self._request is None:\n                    self._request = \"deferred\"\n                return None\n            if self._request == \"delivered-between-checks\":\n                # Less than a window has passed since delivery: start it now.\n                self._request = \"delivered\"\n                check[\"request_window_started\"] = True\n                return None\n            if self._request in (None, \"deferred\"):\n                self._request = \"delivered\"\n                return self._record(self._checkpoint_request(interrupted=list(in_flight.values())))\n            self._mark_answered(False, \"unanswered\")\n            window = _duration(self.window_seconds)\n            return self._record(\n                Action(\n                    \"stop\",\n                    f\"no commit, file change, or new command result in two consecutive \"\n                    f\"{window} windows; the checkpoint request went unanswered\",\n                    \"no-progress-stop\",\n                )\n            )\n\n    def _mark_answered(self, answered: bool, by: str) -> None:\n        for message in reversed(self.messages):\n            if message[\"reason_code\"] == \"no-progress\":\n                if \"answered\" not in message:\n                    message.update(answered=answered, answered_by=by)\n                return\n\n    def finish(self, *, ended_normally: bool) -> None:\n        \"\"\"The executor stopped running; a clean end answers a delivered request.\n\n        A clean end is the executor's own exit 0 (its final report or a\n        `BLOCKED:` line ends the turn); a timeout, interruption, or failure\n        leaves the request unanswered.\n        \"\"\"\n        with self._lock:\n            if self._request in (\"delivered\", \"delivered-between-checks\"):\n                if ended_normally:\n                    self._mark_answered(True, \"lane-ended\")\n                else:\n                    self._mark_answered(False, \"lane-failed\")\n            self._request = None\n\n    def _record(self, action: Action | None) -> Action | None:\n        if action is None:\n            return None\n        if action.kind == \"stop\":\n            self.stop_reason = action.text\n        else:\n            self.messages.append({\"reason_code\": action.reason_code, \"text\": action.text})\n        return action\n\n    def snapshot(self, now: float) -> dict[str, Any]:\n        with self._lock:\n            since = None if self._last_progress_at is None else now - self._last_progress_at\n            return {\n                \"window_seconds\": self.window_seconds,\n                \"seconds_since_progress_seen\": (\n                    None if since is None else round(max(0.0, since), 3)\n                ),\n                \"checkpoint_request\": self._request,\n            }\n\n    def receipt(self) -> dict[str, Any]:\n        with self._lock:\n            return {\n                \"window_seconds\": self.window_seconds,\n                \"window_source\": self.window_source,\n                \"stop_enabled\": self.window_seconds > 0,\n                \"checks\": list(self.checks),\n                \"messages\": [dict(message) for message in self.messages],\n                \"stop_reason\": self.stop_reason,\n            }\n",

    "prompt_content": "\"\"\"Task identity, Codex command construction, and result-store persistence.\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport errno\nimport hashlib\nimport json\nimport os\nimport re\nimport shutil\nimport tempfile\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any, Mapping, Sequence\n\n\ndef _load_repo_runtime_bootstrap():\n    pathlib, sys = __import__(\"pathlib\"), __import__(\"sys\")\n    marker = (\"scripts\", \"adapter_lib.py\")\n    parents = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents\n    root = next((p for p in parents if p.joinpath(*marker).is_file()), None)\n    if root is not None and str(root) not in sys.path:\n        sys.path.insert(0, str(root))\n\n\n_load_repo_runtime_bootstrap()\n\nfrom scripts.runtime_bootstrap import runtime_root  # noqa: E402\nfrom scripts.task_run import task_run_git as _git_owner  # noqa: E402\nfrom scripts.task_run.task_run_contract import (  # noqa: E402\n    _TASK_ID_RE,\n    FAIL,\n    SCHEMA_VERSION,\n    TASK_EFFORTS,\n    TASK_MODEL,\n    TASK_MUSE_EFFORTS,\n    TaskRunError,\n)\n\n\ndef _resolve_lane_executable(\n    value: str, *, empty_error: str, not_runnable: str, not_on_path: str\n) -> str:\n    \"\"\"The one executable-resolution core; wrappers only name their executor.\"\"\"\n    if not value.strip():\n        raise TaskRunError(empty_error)\n    if \"/\" in value:\n        candidate = Path(value).expanduser().resolve()\n        if not candidate.is_file() or not os.access(candidate, os.X_OK):\n            raise TaskRunError(f\"{not_runnable}: {value}\")\n        return str(candidate)\n    resolved = shutil.which(value)\n    if resolved is None:\n        raise TaskRunError(f\"{not_on_path}: {value}\")\n    return resolved\n\n\ndef _resolve_codex(value: str) -> str:\n    return _resolve_lane_executable(\n        value,\n        empty_error=\"--codex must name an executable\",\n        not_runnable=\"Codex executable is not runnable\",\n        not_on_path=\"Codex executable is not on PATH\",\n    )\n\n\ndef resolve_executor_executable(value: str, *, executor: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Resolve the lane executable for the selected executor.\"\"\"\n    return _resolve_lane_executable(\n        value,\n        empty_error=f\"{executor} executor must name an executable\",\n        not_runnable=f\"{executor} executable is not runnable\",\n        not_on_path=f\"{executor} executable is not on PATH\",\n    )\n\n\ndef validate_lane_id(lane: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Validate the safe identifier used by shorthand lane mode.\"\"\"\n    value = lane.strip()\n    if not _TASK_ID_RE.fullmatch(value):\n        raise TaskRunError(\n            \"--lane must be a non-empty id starting with a letter or digit and \"\n            \"containing only letters, digits, dot, underscore, or dash (maximum 96 characters)\"\n        )\n    return value\n\n\ndef _task_id(branch: str, requested: str | None) -> str:\n    if requested is not None:\n        task_id = requested.strip()\n        if not _TASK_ID_RE.fullmatch(task_id):\n            raise TaskRunError(\n                \"--task-id must start with a letter or digit and contain only letters, \"\n                \"digits, dot, underscore, or dash (maximum 96 characters)\"\n            )\n        return task_id\n    slug = re.sub(r\"[^A-Za-z0-9._-]+\", \"-\", branch.replace(\"/\", \"-\")).strip(\"-\")\n    digest = hashlib.sha256(branch.encode(\"utf-8\")).hexdigest()[:8]\n    return f\"{slug[:87] or 'task'}-{digest}\"\n\n\n#: Network for executor lanes (operator, 2026-10-20). Codex: the\n#: workspace-write sandbox's own network switch. Muse: `--sandbox-network`\n#: (`restricted|enabled|proxy-only`, default proxy-only).\nCODEX_LANE_NETWORK_CONFIG = \"sandbox_workspace_write.network_access=true\"\nMUSE_LANE_SANDBOX_NETWORK = \"enabled\"\n\n\ndef build_codex_args(\n    *,\n    effort: str,\n    writable_dirs: Sequence[Path] = (),\n) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"Build Codex host arguments with the task runner's fixed Luna model.\"\"\"\n    # Lanes run loopback servers and fetches in their tests; the operator allowed\n    # network for task-run lanes (2026-10-20). Lane worktrees carry no credentials.\n    # The key configures only workspace-write; the read-only reviewers (self-review,\n    # brief critique) drop it with `read_only_codex` so their argv is unchanged. It\n    # follows `-m` with the other `-c` overrides, so a steer resume moves it\n    # into the `exec resume` parser scope with them (see ef7c520ed).\n    args: list[str] = [\"--sandbox\", \"workspace-write\"]\n    for writable_dir in writable_dirs:\n        args.extend([\"--add-dir\", str(writable_dir.resolve())])\n    args.extend([\"-m\", TASK_MODEL])\n    if effort not in TASK_EFFORTS:\n        allowed = \", \".join(TASK_EFFORTS)\n        raise TaskRunError(f\"--effort must be one of: {allowed} (codex executor)\")\n    args.extend([\"-c\", f\"model_reasoning_effort={effort}\", \"-c\", CODEX_LANE_NETWORK_CONFIG])\n    return args\n\n\ndef read_only_codex(command: list[str]) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"Turn a lane's Codex argv into a read-only reviewer's: no write, no lane network.\"\"\"\n    command[command.index(\"--sandbox\") + 1] = \"read-only\"\n    for index in range(len(command) - 1):\n        if command[index] == \"-c\" and command[index + 1] == CODEX_LANE_NETWORK_CONFIG:\n            del command[index : index + 2]\n            break\n    return command\n\n\ndef build_codex_command(\n    executable: str,\n    *,\n    effort: str,\n    writable_dirs: Sequence[Path] = (),\n) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"Build the Codex command; the task prompt is supplied on stdin.\"\"\"\n    return [\n        executable,\n        \"exec\",\n        *build_codex_args(effort=effort, writable_dirs=writable_dirs),\n        \"-\",\n    ]\n\n\ndef build_muse_args(\n    *,\n    effort: str,\n    prompt_file: Path,\n    worktree: Path,\n) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"Build Muse host arguments with the task runner's curated effort preset.\n\n    `muse exec` takes no stdin prompt, so the lane prompt travels via\n    `--prompt-file`. Approval prompts cannot be answered headless, so they are\n    disabled; the OS sandbox stays on (the Codex lane's workspace-write\n    equivalent) with its network enabled like the Codex lane's. `muse exec` honors a single effective workspace (the last\n    `--workspace` wins), so the lane worktree root is passed exactly once:\n    passing every writable dir rooted the lane at `<worktree>/.agents` and\n    every write outside it was refused (#814). The lane worktree is created\n    for the agent, so it is trusted: otherwise the repo's rules are skipped\n    and delegation stays unavailable. `--json` streams machine-readable\n    events on stdout: without it the lane stdout stays empty until the run\n    ends, so a timed-out lane receipted no phases and no retry cause (#886).\n    The terminal report is extracted from the `run_terminal` event, separate\n    from that JSONL transport.\n    \"\"\"\n    if effort not in TASK_MUSE_EFFORTS:\n        allowed = \", \".join(TASK_MUSE_EFFORTS)\n        raise TaskRunError(f\"--effort must be one of: {allowed} (muse executor)\")\n    return [\n        \"--json\",\n        \"--reasoning-effort\",\n        effort,\n        \"--disable-approval\",\n        \"--sandbox-network\",\n        MUSE_LANE_SANDBOX_NETWORK,\n        \"--trust-workspace\",\n        \"--workspace\",\n        str(worktree),\n        \"--prompt-file\",\n        str(prompt_file),\n    ]\n\n\ndef build_muse_command(\n    executable: str,\n    *,\n    effort: str,\n    prompt_file: Path,\n    worktree: Path,\n) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"Build the Muse command; the task prompt travels via --prompt-file.\"\"\"\n    return [\n        executable,\n        \"exec\",\n        *build_muse_args(\n            effort=effort, prompt_file=prompt_file, worktree=worktree\n        ),\n    ]\n\n\n#: Env-name fragments (case-insensitive) treated as secret-bearing (#832).\n#: The lane child inherits ambient env, and an executor may re-emit its\n#: environment where any local process can read it (observed: muse\n#: `--env-json` argv). Names only are ever matched or recorded, never values.\n_SECRET_ENV_FRAGMENTS = (\n    \"API_KEY\",\n    \"APIKEY\",\n    \"_TOKEN\",\n    \"TOKEN_\",\n    \"SECRET\",\n    \"PASSWD\",\n    \"PASSWORD\",\n    \"PRIVATE_KEY\",\n    \"CREDENTIALS\",\n    \"ACCESS_TOKEN\",\n    \"AUTH_TOKEN\",\n    \"REFRESH_TOKEN\",\n    \"SESSION_TOKEN\",\n    \"CLIENT_SECRET\",\n)\n_SECRET_ENV_EXACT = frozenset({\"TOKEN\", \"API_KEY\", \"SECRET\", \"PASSWORD\"})\n\n#: Executor auth keys that must stay in the lane env to function (#832).\n#: Muse authenticates from its stored credentials; codex reads its key here.\n_EXECUTOR_SECRET_KEEPS: dict[str, frozenset[str]] = {\n    \"codex\": frozenset({\"OPENAI_API_KEY\"}),\n    \"muse\": frozenset(),\n}\n\n#: Operator override: comma-separated exact env names to keep in lane env.\nLANE_KEEP_SECRET_ENV = \"CHARNESS_TASK_RUN_KEEP_SECRET_ENV\"\n\n\ndef _is_secret_env_name(name: str) -> bool:\n    upper = name.upper()\n    return upper in _SECRET_ENV_EXACT or any(\n        fragment in upper for fragment in _SECRET_ENV_FRAGMENTS\n    )\n\n\ndef scrubbed_lane_env(\n    payload: dict[str, Any],\n    env: Mapping[str, str],\n    executor: str,\n) -> dict[str, str]:\n    \"\"\"Drop secret-pattern names from the lane child env, recording names (#832).\n\n    Unknown executors keep nothing secret-patterned (fail closed). Only the\n    executor's auth keeps and explicit `CHARNESS_TASK_RUN_KEEP_SECRET_ENV`\n    names survive; the receipt records scrubbed/kept names, never values.\n    \"\"\"\n    keeps = set(_EXECUTOR_SECRET_KEEPS.get(executor, frozenset()))\n    for extra in os.environ.get(LANE_KEEP_SECRET_ENV, \"\").split(\",\"):\n        if extra.strip():\n            keeps.add(extra.strip())\n    scrubbed = sorted(\n        name for name in env if _is_secret_env_name(name) and name not in keeps\n    )\n    kept = sorted(\n        name for name in env if _is_secret_env_name(name) and name in keeps\n    )\n    payload[\"lane_env\"] = {\n        \"executor\": executor,\n        \"scrubbed\": scrubbed,\n        \"kept_secret_names\": kept,\n    }\n    return {name: value for name, value in env.items() if name not in scrubbed}\n\n\ndef task_runtime_root(repo_root: Path) -> Path:\n    \"\"\"Resolve the result store by clean parent identity, ignoring ambient roots.\"\"\"\n    preview_env = os.environ.copy()\n    for key in (\"CHARNESS_RUNTIME_ROOT\", \"CHARNESS_RUNTIME_ROOT_AUTO\", \"CHARNESS_RUNTIME_REPO_KEY\"):\n        preview_env.pop(key, None)\n    return runtime_root(repo_root, preview_env)\n\n\ndef task_result_path(runtime_path: Path, task_id: str) -> Path:\n    if not _TASK_ID_RE.fullmatch(task_id):\n        raise TaskRunError(f\"invalid task id: {task_id!r}\")\n    return runtime_path / \"task-run\" / task_id / \"result.json\"\n\n\ndef task_execution_runtime_root(runtime_path: Path, task_id: str) -> Path:\n    \"\"\"Return the lane-private runtime root beneath one task result directory.\"\"\"\n    return task_result_path(runtime_path, task_id).parent / \"runtime\"\n\n\ndef write_task_result(runtime_path: Path, result: Mapping[str, Any]) -> Path:\n    \"\"\"Atomically publish the sole persisted task-run result.\"\"\"\n    path = task_result_path(runtime_path, str(result[\"task_id\"]))\n    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    fd, temp_name = tempfile.mkstemp(prefix=\".result.\", suffix=\".tmp\", dir=path.parent)\n    temp_path = Path(temp_name)\n    try:\n        with os.fdopen(fd, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as handle:\n            json.dump(dict(result), handle, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)\n            handle.write(\"\\n\")\n            handle.flush()\n            os.fsync(handle.fileno())\n        os.replace(temp_path, path)\n    finally:\n        temp_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)\n    return path\n\n\ndef utc_now_iso() -> str:\n    \"\"\"Wall-clock stamp for the receipt; the runtime's own clock, never a test's.\"\"\"\n    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec=\"seconds\").replace(\"+00:00\", \"Z\")\n\n\ndef runner_liveness(record: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Advisory pid check on the runner that wrote this record.\n\n    A lane's record read `timed-out` while its runner was visibly alive (#791),\n    and the record could not say which reading was stale because it carried no\n    process identity. `alive` is `os.kill(pid, 0)` at read time, never\n    persisted, and only advisory: a pid can be reused, and a live runner on a\n    terminal record is normal while the runner finishes retention after its\n    final write. A `running` record whose pid is dead is the one firm reading:\n    that record is stale.\n    \"\"\"\n    pid = record.get(\"runner_pid\")\n    if isinstance(pid, bool) or not isinstance(pid, int) or pid <= 0:\n        return {\"runner_pid\": None, \"alive\": None}\n    try:\n        os.kill(pid, 0)\n        alive = True\n    except ProcessLookupError:\n        alive = False\n    except PermissionError:\n        alive = True\n    except OverflowError:\n        alive = None\n    except OSError as exc:\n        if exc.errno not in {errno.EINVAL, errno.EOVERFLOW}:\n            raise\n        alive = None\n    return {\"runner_pid\": pid, \"alive\": alive}\n\n\ndef read_task_result(runtime_path: Path, task_id: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:\n    path = task_result_path(runtime_path, task_id)\n    if not path.is_file():\n        return None\n    payload = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n    if not isinstance(payload, dict):\n        raise TaskRunError(f\"task result must be a JSON object: {path}\")\n    return payload\n\n\ndef read_task_results(runtime_path: Path) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:\n    root = runtime_path / \"task-run\"\n    results: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n    for path in sorted(root.glob(\"*/result.json\")) if root.is_dir() else []:\n        payload = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n        if not isinstance(payload, dict):\n            raise TaskRunError(f\"task result must be a JSON object: {path}\")\n        results.append(payload)\n    return results\n\n\ndef task_status(repo_root: Path, task_id: str | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Read this repo's task-run result store without mutation.\"\"\"\n    resolved_repo = _git_owner._require_git_root(repo_root)\n    runtime_path = task_runtime_root(resolved_repo)\n    if task_id is not None:\n        record = read_task_result(runtime_path, task_id)\n        if record is not None:\n            return {**record, \"liveness\": runner_liveness(record)}\n        return {\n            \"schema_version\": SCHEMA_VERSION,\n            \"event\": \"task-status\",\n            \"repo_root\": str(resolved_repo),\n            \"task_id\": task_id,\n            \"status\": \"missing\",\n            \"result_path\": str(task_result_path(runtime_path, task_id)),\n        }\n    return {\n        \"schema_version\": SCHEMA_VERSION,\n        \"event\": \"task-status-list\",\n        \"repo_root\": str(resolved_repo),\n        \"runtime_root\": str(runtime_path),\n        \"tasks\": [\n            {**record, \"liveness\": runner_liveness(record)}\n            for record in read_task_results(runtime_path)\n        ],\n    }\n\n\ndef _failure_payload(\n    *,\n    repo_root: Path | None,\n    target_path: Path | None,\n    task_id: str | None,\n    error: str,\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    return {\n        \"schema_version\": SCHEMA_VERSION,\n        \"event\": \"task-run\",\n        \"status\": FAIL,\n        \"phase\": \"preflight\",\n        \"approval_eligibility\": \"ineligible\",\n        \"repo_root\": str(repo_root) if repo_root is not None else None,\n        \"worktree_path\": str(target_path) if target_path is not None else None,\n        \"task_id\": task_id,\n        \"error\": error,\n        \"next_step\": \"Fix the preflight error, then rerun task run from a clean parent with an unused worktree path.\",\n    }\n\n\ndef _runtime_preview(repo_root: Path) -> Path:\n    return task_runtime_root(repo_root)\n",

sys.path.insert(0, str(root))\n\n\n_load_repo_runtime_bootstrap()\n\n\ndef run_self_review(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Run a read-only Codex self-review and retain its     \"unavailable-skip\",\n            reason=\"a read-only Codex reviewer is unavailable for the selected lane executor\",\n        )\n    original_command = executor_info.get(\"command\")\n    if   command = task_run_runtime.read_only_codex(\n            task_run_runtime.build_codex_command(executable, effort=\"medium\")\n        )\n        command[-1:-1] = [\n            \"--json\", \"charness.task_self_review.v1\"\n_SELF_REVIEW_SCHEMA = {\n    \"type\": \"object\",\n    \"properties\": {\n        \"kind\": {\"type\": \"string\", \"enum\": [_SELF_REVIEW_KIND]},\n \"string\"}},\n                },\n                \"required\": [\"summary\", \"disposition\", \"evidence\"],\n                \"additionalProperties\": False,\n            },\n        },\n    },\n    \"required\": [\"kind\", \"findings\"],\n    \"additionalProperties\": False,\n}\n\n\ndef self_review_block(\n    state: str,\n    *,\n    findings: object = (),\n    reason: str | None = None,\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Normalize self-review findings without turning a skip into a pass.\"\"\"\n    if state not in SELF_REVIEW_STATES:\n        raise ValueError(f\"unknown self-review state: {state}\")\n    grouped: dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]] = {key: [] for key in SELF_REVIEW_DISPOSITIONS}\n    if state == \"findings-received\":\n        if not isinstance(findings, (list, tuple)):\n            raise ValueError(\"self-review findings must be a list\")\n        for item in findings:\n            if not isinstance(item, Mapping) or item.get(\"disposition\") not in grouped:\n                raise ValueError(\"self-review finding needs fixed, deferred, or disputed disposition\")\n            summary, disposition = item.get(\"summary\"), item[\"disposition\"]\n            if not                raise ValueError(\"self-review finding evidence must be a list of strings\")\n            finding[\"evidence\"] = [value for value in evidence if value.strip()]\n            grouped[disposition].append(finding)\n    return {\n        \"kind\": _SELF_REVIEW_KIND,\n        \"state\": state,\n        \"findings\": grouped,\n        **({\"reason\": reason.strip()} if reason     "prompt_content": "from __future__ import annotations\n\nimport hashlib\nimport re\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any, Callable\n\n_DEFAULT_BRANCHES = frozenset({\"main\", \"master\", \"trunk\", \"develop\"})\n\n\ndef _head_branch(repo_root: Path) -> str | None:\n    \"\"\"The checked-out branch, read from the git files (no process spawn).\"\"\"\n    dot_git = repo_root / \".git\"\n    try:\n        if dot_git.is_file():\n            text = dot_git.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").strip()\n            if not text.startswith(\"gitdir:\"):\n                return None\n            git_dir = Path(text[len(\"gitdir:\"):].strip())\n            dot_git = git_dir if git_dir.is_absolute() else repo_root / git_dir\n        head = (dot_git / \"HEAD\").read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").strip()\n    except OSError:\n        return None\n    prefix = \"ref: refs/heads/\"\n    return head[len(prefix):] if head.startswith(prefix) else None\n\n\ndef lane_token(repo_root: Path) -> str | None:\n    \"\"\"A name part no parallel lane shares: the task-run lane id, else the branch.\n\n    Follow-up records were numbered by counting files in the lane's own\n    worktree, so parallel lanes picked the same number and their merges\n    collided as add/add conflicts. Every non-default branch yields a readable\n    slug (the lane id for `task/<lane>`, or `branch` when nothing of the name\n    survives slugging) plus an 8-hex hash of the raw\n    branch. The slug alone is lossy (`foo_bar`, `Foo`, and a plain `foo-bar`\n    all alias `task/foo-bar`), and any exception for \"already a slug\" names\n    lets a crafted name take another branch's token, so two branches share a\n    record only on a hash-prefix collision. On a default branch or a\n    detached HEAD there is no lane identity, and the counter stays.\n    \"\"\"\n    branch = _head_branch(repo_root)\n    # A reftable repository's HEAD names the placeholder `.invalid` for every\n    # branch, so it carries no lane identity either.\n    if not branch or branch in _DEFAULT_BRANCHES or branch == \".invalid\":\n        return None\n    token = branch[len(\"task/\"):] if branch.startswith(\"task/\") else branch\n    slug = re.sub(r\"[^a-z0-9]+\", \"-\", token.lower()).strip(\"-\")\n    digest = hashlib.sha256(branch.encode(\"utf-8\")).hexdigest()[:8]\n    return f\"{slug or 'branch'}-{digest}\"\n\n\ndef resolved_followup_record_payload(\n    repo_root: Path,\n    *,\n    adapter: dict[str, object],\n    resolved_title: str,\n    artifact_date: Any,\n    current_pointer_target_path: str | None,\n    reuse_subject_key: str | None = None,\n    scaffold_artifact_lib: Any,\n    resolve_artifact_path: Any,\n    resolution: Callable[[Path], str],\n) -> dict[str, object]:\n    def record_payload_for(title_text: str) -> dict[str, object]:\n        return resolve_artifact_path.payload_for(\n            repo_root,\n            \"debug\",\n            title_text,\n            intent=\"record\",\n            artifact_date=artifact_date,\n            adapter=adapter,\n        )\n\n    current_target = current_pointer_target_path or \"\"\n\n    def usable(candidate_path: str) -> bool:\n        if candidate_path == current_target:\n            return False\n        if (\n            reuse_subject_key is not None\n            and scaffold_artifact_lib.record_subject_slug(candidate_path) == reuse_subject_key\n            and resolution(repo_root / candidate_path) != \"resolved\"\n        ):\n            return True\n        return not (repo_root / candidate_path).exists()\n\n    token = lane_token(repo_root)\n    # On a lane branch even the first name carries the lane token: parallel\n    # lanes would otherwise both take the plain dated name and collide.\n    candidate = record_payload_for(resolved_title)\n    if token is None and usable(str(candidate[\"write_artifact_path\"])):\n        return candidate\n    base = f\"followup-{token}\" if token else \"followup\"\n    for suffix in (base, f\"{base}-2\", f\"{base}-3\", f\"{base}-4\"):\n        candidate = record_payload_for(f\"{resolved_title} {suffix}\")\n        if usable(str(candidate[\"write_artifact_path\"])):\n            return candidate\n    raise SystemExit(\n        \"resolved current debug artifact needs a fresh dated follow-up record, but every deterministic \"\n        \"default slug for today already exists; rerun scaffold_debug_artifact.py with --title <specific follow-up title>\"\n    )\n",

    "carrier_path": "charness-artifacts/critique/workers/release-8150-final-followup25/semantic-input/0013-content.bin",
    "carrier_sha256": "8547676c42193d7789900490bfe6afa0747aee19ff4f12754eb38df71139816a",
    "content_sha256": "443963e20ef4afaf1f46f68e0539f7f4284b6202d01b6899d30e302da2f38291",
    "disposition": "present",
    "path": "tests/charness_cli/test_task_run_event_capture.py",
    "prompt_content": "\"\"\"Event bytes survive every way a lane's executor can end, or the loss is recorded.\n\nThe carrier stamps executor stdout through a pipe (`StampingPipe`). The\nv8.15.0 release critique (R1) found that close() could give up on the reader\nand the caller then closed the sink, dropping bytes silently. These pin the\ncontract: everything written before close reaches the events log\nbyte-for-byte on normal exit, timeout, a signalled child and a parent\ninterrupt; a writer that outlives close, or a failing sink, is reported in\nthe capture receipt and never blocks the executor.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport io\nimport json\nimport os\nimport shutil\nimport threading\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport pytest\n\nfrom scripts.task_run import task_run_event_timing as timing\nfrom scripts.task_run import task_run_execution\n\nfrom .test_task_run_fixtures import _codex, _repo, _run\n\npytestmark = pytest.mark.boundary_contract(\n    reason=\"the claim is what a real executor process leaves in the pipe on timeout, signal and detach\"\n)\n\nSTAMP = \"2026-10-20T08:00:00.000Z\"\nLINES = (\n    b'{\"type\":\"thread.started\",\"thread_id\":\"fake-thread\"}\\n'\n    b'{\"type\":\"item.started\",\"item\":{\"id\":\"c1\",\"type\":\"command_execution\"}}\\n'\n    b\"plain progress text\\n\"\n)\nPARTIAL = b'{\"type\":\"turn'\nSTAMPED = (\n    b'{\"charness_received_at\":\"2026-10-20T08:00:00.000Z\",\"type\":\"thread.started\",'\n    b'\"thread_id\":\"fake-thread\"}\\n'\n    b'{\"charness_received_at\":\"2026-10-20T08:00:00.000Z\",\"type\":\"item.started\",'\n    b'\"item\":{\"id\":\"c1\",\"type\":\"command_execution\"}}\\n'\n    b\"plain progress text\\n\"\n)\n\n\n@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)\ndef _fixed_clock(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:\n    monkeypatch.setattr(timing, \"utc_receive_stamp\", lambda: STAMP)\n\n\ndef _emit_body(tail: str) -> str:\n    # printf %s keeps the bytes exact; the partial line has no newline.\n    payload = (LINES + PARTIAL).decode(\"ascii\").replace(\"'\", \"'\\\\''\")\n    return f\"printf 'VALUE = 2\\\\n' > module.py\\nprintf '%s' '{payload}'\\n{tail}\"\n\n\ndef _events(payload: dict[str, object]) -> bytes:\n    \"\"\"This run's slice of the lane events log (the log appends across runs).\"\"\"\n    window = payload[\"execution\"][\"event_log_window\"]  # type: ignore[index]\n    with Path(window[\"path\"]).open(\"rb\") as handle:\n        handle.seek(window[\"offset_bytes\"])\n        return handle.read(window[\"byte_count\"])\n\n\ndef test_timeout_keeps_every_event_byte_including_the_partial_tail(tmp_path: Path) -> None:\n    repo = _repo(tmp_path)\n    executable = _codex(tmp_path, _emit_body(\"sleep 5\"))\n\n    payload = _run(repo, tmp_path, executable, timeout_seconds=1)\n\n    assert payload[\"status\"] == \"timed-out\"\n    # The retained log ends the partial line with the newline retention adds.\n    assert _events(payload) == STAMPED + PARTIAL + b\"\\n\"\n    assert \"event_capture_incomplete\" not in payload[\"execution\"]\n\n\ndef test_a_signalled_child_keeps_every_event_byte(tmp_path: Path) -> None:\n    repo = _repo(tmp_path)\n    executable = _codex(tmp_path, _emit_body(\"kill -TERM $$\"), deliver=False)\n\n    payload = _run(repo, tmp_path, executable)\n\n    assert payload[\"candidate\"][\"status\"] == \"wip\"\n    assert _events(payload) == STAMPED + PARTIAL + b\"\\n\"\n    assert \"event_capture_incomplete\" not in payload[\"execution\"]\n\n\ndef test_parent_interrupt_drains_the_pipe_before_the_sink_closes(\n    tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch\n) -> None:\n    stdout_log = tmp_path / \"stdout.log\"\n\n    def writing_then_interrupted(*_args, **_kwargs):\n        # fd 1 is the stamping pipe's write end while the executor runs.\n        os.write(1, LINES + PARTIAL)\n        raise KeyboardInterrupt\n\n    monkeypatch.setattr(task_run_execution, \"run_monitored_phase\", writing_then_interrupted)\n\n    result = task_run_execution._execute_codex(\n        [\"codex\"],\n        prompt=\"stop\",\n        target_path=tmp_path,\n        configured_env={},\n        stdout_log=stdout_log,\n        stderr_log=tmp_path / \"stderr.log\",\n        timeout_seconds=1,\n    )\n\n    assert result[\"interrupted\"] is True\n    assert \"event_capture_incomplete\" not in result\n    assert (tmp_path / \"codex.events.log\").read_bytes() == STAMPED + PARTIAL + b\"\\n\"\n\n\ndef test_a_writer_that_outlives_close_is_reported_not_waited_on(\n    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,\n) -> None:\n    monkeypatch.setattr(timing, \"_CLOSE_GRACE_SECONDS\", 0.2)\n    sink = io.BytesIO()\n    pipe = timing.StampingPipe(sink, clock=lambda: STAMP)\n    descendant = os.dup(pipe.fileno())  # a descendant that escaped the group kill\n    try:\n        os.write(descendant, LINES + PARTIAL)\n        pipe.close()\n        capture = pipe.capture()\n    finally:\n        os.close(descendant)\n\n    # Everything written before close is kept; what the survivor writes later\n    # is not, and the receipt says so instead of staying silent.\n    assert sink.getvalue() == STAMPED + PARTIAL\n    assert capture[\"complete\"] is False\n    assert capture[\"writer_open_at_close\"] is True\n    assert capture[\"received_bytes\"] == len(LINES + PARTIAL)\n\n\nclass _FailingSink(io.BytesIO):\n    def write(self, data: bytes) -> int:  # type: ignore[override]\n        raise OSError(28, \"No space left on device\")\n\n\ndef test_a_failing_sink_is_reported_and_never_blocks_the_executor() -> None:\n    pipe = timing.StampingPipe(_FailingSink(), clock=lambda: STAMP)\n    # Far more than a pipe buffer: a reader that stopped draining would block\n    # this writer forever, which is how a failed sink would hang the lane.\n    flood = b'{\"type\":\"item.started\"}\\n' * 40_000\n    writer = threading.Thread(target=os.write, args=(pipe.fileno(), flood), daemon=True)\n    writer.start()\n    writer.join(timeout=10)\n    assert not writer.is_alive()\n    pipe.close()\n\n    capture = pipe.capture()\n    assert capture[\"complete\"] is False\n    assert capture[\"error\"] == \"OSError: [Errno 28] No space left on device\"\n    assert capture[\"received_bytes\"] == len(flood)\n    assert capture[\"dropped_bytes\"] > 0\n\n\nclass _ShortSink(io.BytesIO):\n    def write(self, data: bytes) -> int:  # type: ignore[override]\n        return super().write(bytes(data)[:-1]) if len(data) > 1 else super().write(data)\n\n\ndef test_a_short_sink_write_is_recorded_as_lost_bytes() -> None:\n    \"\"\"A sink that accepts only part of a line must not leave capture 'complete'\n    (release critique follow-up R-002).\"\"\"\n    sink = _ShortSink()\n    pipe = timing.StampingPipe(sink, clock=lambda: STAMP)\n    os.write(pipe.fileno(), b'{\"type\":\"a\"}\\n{\"type\":\"b\"}\\n')\n    pipe.close()\n\n    capture = pipe.capture()\n    assert capture[\"complete\"] is False\n    assert capture[\"error\"].startswith(\"short write: \")\n    assert capture[\"dropped_bytes\"] > 0\n\n\ndef test_incomplete_capture_reaches_the_execution_record(\n    tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch\n) -> None:\n    if shutil.which(\"setsid\") is None:\n        pytest.skip(\"setsid is unavailable\")\n    monkeypatch.setattr(timing, \"_CLOSE_GRACE_SECONDS\", 0.2)\n    repo = _repo(tmp_path)\n    # A detached descendant keeps stdout open past the process-group kill; the\n    # executor exits only once it has left the group, so the kill cannot win.\n    ready = tmp_path / \"detached\"\n    executable = _codex(\n        tmp_path,\n        _emit_body(\n            f\"setsid sh -c 'touch {ready}; exec sleep 3' &\\n\"\n            f\"while [ ! -e {ready} ]; do :; done\"\n        ),\n    )\n\n    payload = _run(repo, tmp_path, executable)\n\n    gaps = payload[\"execution\"][\"event_capture_incomplete\"]\n    assert [(gap[\"invocation\"], gap[\"writer_open_at_close\"]) for gap in gaps] == [(0, True)]\n    assert _events(payload).startswith(STAMPED + PARTIAL)\n    json.dumps(payload)  # the receipt stays serializable\n\n\nclass _HungSink(io.BytesIO):\n    \"\"\"A sink whose first write blocks until the test releases it.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self) -> None:\n        super().__init__()\n        self.entered = threading.Event()\n        self.release = threading.Event()\n\n    def write(self, data: bytes) -> int:  # type: ignore[override]\n        self.entered.set()\n        self.release.wait(timeout=30)\n        return super().write(data)\n\n\ndef test_a_hung_sink_cannot_hang_close_or_receive_writes_after_it(\n    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,\n) -> None:\n    monkeypatch.setattr(timing, \"_CLOSE_GRACE_SECONDS\", 0.05)\n    sink = _HungSink()\n    pipe = timing.StampingPipe(sink, clock=lambda: STAMP)\n    os.write(pipe.fileno(), LINES)\n    assert sink.entered.wait(timeout=30)\n\n    pipe.close()  # returns although the reader is stuck in the sink\n    capture = pipe.capture()\n    sink.release.set()\n    pipe._thread.join(timeout=30)\n\n    assert capture[\"complete\"] is False\n    assert capture[\"error\"] == \"event reader did not finish before close\"\n    # The stuck write finishes; every later line is dropped and counted.\n    assert pipe.capture()[\"dropped_bytes\"] > 0\n    assert sink.getvalue().count(b\"\\n\") == 1\n\n\ndef test_a_failing_clock_keeps_the_bytes_unstamped_and_records_it() -> None:\n    def broken_clock() -> str:\n        raise ValueError(\"clock broke\")\n\n    sink = io.BytesIO()\n    pipe = timing.StampingPipe(sink, clock=broken_clock)\n    os.write(pipe.fileno(), LINES + PARTIAL)\n    pipe.close()\n\n    assert sink.getvalue() == LINES + PARTIAL\n    assert pipe.capture()[\"error\"] == \"ValueError: clock broke\"\n\n\ndef test_a_failing_pipe_read_is_recorded(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:\n    def failing_select(*_args: object) -> object:\n        raise OSError(\"select failed\")\n\n    # Rebinds only the timing module's name, not the process-wide select module.\n    monkeypatch.setattr(timing, \"select\", type(\"S\", (), {\"select\": staticmethod(failing_select)}))\n    pipe = timing.StampingPipe(io.BytesIO(), clock=lambda: STAMP)\n    pipe.close()\n\n    assert pipe.capture()[\"error\"] == \"OSError: select failed\"\n    assert pipe.capture()[\"complete\"] is False\n\n\ndef test_the_observer_sees_complete_events_and_its_faults_cost_no_bytes() -> None:\n    seen: list[dict[str, object]] = []\n\n    def observer(event: dict[str, object]) -> None:\n        seen.append(event)\n        raise RuntimeError(\"observer bug\")\n\n    sink = io.BytesIO()\n    pipe = timing.StampingPipe(sink, clock=lambda: STAMP, observer=observer)\n    os.write(pipe.fileno(), LINES + b\"{broken json\\n\" + PARTIAL)\n    pipe.close()\n\n    assert sink.getvalue() == STAMPED + b\"{broken json\\n\" + PARTIAL\n    # Two JSON objects reach the observer; text, broken JSON and the\n    # unfinished line do not, and its exceptions are contained.\n    assert [event[\"type\"] for event in seen] == [\"thread.started\", \"item.started\"]\n    assert pipe.capture()[\"complete\"] is True\n",

    "prompt_content": "\"\"\"Receive-time stamps on lane events logs, and the command-timing reader.\n\nCodex items and Muse envelopes carry no wall time, so the carrier stamps each\nJSON line as it arrives on the executor's stdout pipe. Every clock here is\ninjected; no test reads wall time or waits on a deadline.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nimport io\nimport json\nimport os\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport pytest\n\nfrom scripts.cli import cmd_task as task_payload\nfrom scripts.task_run import task_run, task_run_runtime\nfrom scripts.task_run import task_run_event_timing as timing\n\nFIELD = timing.RECEIVED_AT_FIELD\n\n\ndef _codex(at: str, kind: str, item: dict[str, object]) -> str:\n    return json.dumps({FIELD: at, \"type\": kind, \"item\": item})\n\n\ndef _muse(at: str, payload_type: str, payload: dict[str, object]) -> str:\n    return json.dumps({FIELD: at, \"payload_type\": payload_type, \"payload\": payload})\n\n\ndef test_stamp_inserts_field_first_and_keeps_event_bytes() -> None:\n    line = b'{\"type\":\"item.started\",\"item\":{\"id\":\"item_1\"}}\\n'\n    stamped = timing.stamp_event_line(line, \"2026-10-20T08:00:00.000Z\")\n\n    assert stamped == (\n        b'{\"charness_received_at\":\"2026-10-20T08:00:00.000Z\",'\n        b'\"type\":\"item.started\",\"item\":{\"id\":\"item_1\"}}\\n'\n    )\n    assert timing.stamp_event_line(b\"{}\\n\", \"t\") == b'{\"charness_received_at\":\"t\"}\\n'\n    for untouched in (b\"plain agent text\\n\", b\"[1, 2]\\n\", b\"{not json\\n\", stamped):\n        assert timing.stamp_event_line(untouched, \"t\") == untouched\n\n\ndef test_stamping_pipe_stamps_each_line_with_the_injected_clock() -> None:\n    sink = io.BytesIO()\n    # One clock read per received line; the plain-text line passes through.\n    stamps = iter(\n        [\"2026-10-20T08:00:00.000Z\", \"2026-10-20T08:00:00.700Z\", \"2026-10-20T08:00:01.500Z\"]\n    )\n    pipe = timing.StampingPipe(sink, clock=lambda: next(stamps))\n    os.write(pipe.fileno(), b'{\"type\":\"thread.started\"}\\nCONTRACT-READ\\n{\"type\":\"turn.started\"}')\n    pipe.close()\n\n    assert sink.getvalue() == (\n        b'{\"charness_received_at\":\"2026-10-20T08:00:00.000Z\",\"type\":\"thread.started\"}\\n'\n        b\"CONTRACT-READ\\n\"\n        b'{\"charness_received_at\":\"2026-10-20T08:00:01.500Z\",\"type\":\"turn.started\"}'\n    )\n\n\ndef test_codex_command_timing_reports_durations_families_and_phases() -> None:\n    test_cmd = \"/usr/bin/zsh -lc 'pnpm run test:changed'\"\n    lint_cmd = \"/usr/bin/zsh -lc 'pnpm run lint && pnpm run check:types'\"\n    lines = [\n        _codex(\"2026-10-20T08:00:00.000Z\", \"item.completed\",\n               {\"id\": \"m1\", \"type\": \"agent_message\", \"text\": \"CONTRACT-READ\"}),\n        _codex(\"2026-10-20T08:01:00.000Z\", \"item.started\",\n               {\"id\": \"c1\", \"type\": \"command_execution\", \"command\": \"cat gateway/test/a.ts\"}),\n        _codex(\"2026-10-20T08:01:02.000Z\", \"item.completed\",\n               {\"id\": \"c1\", \"type\": \"command_execution\", \"command\": \"cat gateway/test/a.ts\"}),\n        _codex(\"2026-10-20T08:02:00.000Z\", \"item.completed\",\n               {\"id\": \"m2\", \"type\": \"agent_message\", \"text\": \"EDITING\"}),\n        _codex(\"2026-10-20T08:05:00.000Z\", \"item.completed\",\n               {\"id\": \"m3\", \"type\": \"agent_message\", \"text\": \"TESTING\"}),\n        _codex(\"2026-10-20T08:05:00.000Z\", \"item.started\",\n               {\"id\": \"c2\", \"type\": \"command_execution\", \"command\": test_cmd}),\n        _codex(\"2026-10-20T08:17:30.250Z\", \"item.completed\",\n               {\"id\": \"c2\", \"type\": \"command_execution\", \"command\": test_cmd}),\n        _codex(\"2026-10-20T08:18:00.000Z\", \"item.started\",\n               {\"id\": \"c3\", \"type\": \"command_execution\", \"command\": lint_cmd}),\n    ]\n\n    report = timing.command_timing(\"\\n\".join(lines) + \"\\n\")\n\n    assert report[\"stamped\"] is True\n    assert report[\"span_seconds\"] == 1080.0\n    assert report[\"command_starts\"] == 3\n    assert report[\"starts_by_family\"] == {\"test\": 1, \"lint\": 1, \"typecheck\": 1, \"merge\": 0}\n    assert report[\"untimed_commands\"] == 1\n    assert report[\"longest_commands\"] == [\n        {\"command\": test_cmd, \"duration_seconds\": 750.25, \"families\": [\"test\"]},\n        {\"command\": \"cat gateway/test/a.ts\", \"duration_seconds\": 2.0, \"families\": []},\n    ]\n    assert [(p[\"phase\"], p[\"seconds\"]) for p in report[\"phases\"]] == [\n        (\"CONTRACT-READ\", 120.0),\n        (\"EDITING\", 180.0),\n        (\"TESTING\", 780.0),\n    ]\n\n\ndef test_a_resumed_codex_session_reusing_item_ids_counts_separate_commands() -> None:\n    \"\"\"A preempted Codex lane resumes the same session, which numbers items from\n    `item_1` again in the same events log (claims review B2): the reader must\n    not merge the killed command with the resumed one.\"\"\"\n    killed = \"/usr/bin/zsh -lc 'pnpm run test:changed'\"\n    rerun = \"/usr/bin/zsh -lc 'pnpm run lint'\"\n    lines = [\n        _codex(\"2026-10-20T08:00:00.000Z\", \"item.started\",\n               {\"id\": \"item_1\", \"type\": \"command_execution\", \"command\": killed}),\n        _codex(\"2026-10-20T08:20:00.000Z\", \"item.started\",\n               {\"id\": \"item_1\", \"type\": \"command_execution\", \"command\": rerun}),\n        _codex(\"2026-10-20T08:20:05.000Z\", \"item.completed\",\n               {\"id\": \"item_1\", \"type\": \"command_execution\", \"command\": rerun}),\n    ]\n\n    report = timing.command_timing(\"\\n\".join(lines) + \"\\n\")\n\n    assert report[\"command_starts\"] == 2\n    assert report[\"starts_by_family\"][\"test\"] == 1 and report[\"starts_by_family\"][\"lint\"] == 1\n    assert report[\"untimed_commands\"] == 1\n    assert report[\"longest_commands\"] == [\n        {\"command\": rerun, \"duration_seconds\": 5.0, \"families\": [\"lint\"]},\n    ]\n\n\ndef test_muse_command_timing_pairs_bash_task_lifecycle() -> None:\n    def task(at: str, kind: str, task_id: str, **event: object) -> str:\n        return _muse(at, f\"task.lifecycle.{kind}\", {\"task_id\": task_id, \"event\": event})\n\n    chunk = json.dumps({\"command\": \"git merge main\", \"exit_code\": 0})\n    lines = [\n        _muse(\"2026-10-20T08:00:00.000Z\", \"run.output.delta\", {\"text\": \"CONTRACT-\"}),\n        _muse(\"2026-10-20T08:00:01.000Z\", \"run.output.delta\", {\"text\": \"READ\\n\"}),\n        task(\"2026-10-20T08:00:02.000Z\", \"proposed\", \"t1\", task_kind=\"tool.bash\"),\n        task(\"2026-10-20T08:00:03.000Z\", \"started\", \"t1\"),\n        task(\"2026-10-20T08:00:09.000Z\", \"output\", \"t1\", chunk=chunk),\n        task(\"2026-10-20T08:00:09.000Z\", \"completed\", \"t1\"),\n        task(\"2026-10-20T08:00:10.000Z\", \"proposed\", \"r1\", task_kind=\"model.meta.response\"),\n        task(\"2026-10-20T08:00:10.000Z\", \"started\", \"r1\"),\n    ]\n\n    report = timing.command_timing(\"\\n\".join(lines))\n\n    assert report[\"command_starts\"] == 1\n    assert report[\"starts_by_family\"][\"merge\"] == 1\n    assert report[\"longest_commands\"] == [\n        {\"command\": \"git merge main\", \"duration_seconds\": 6.0, \"families\": [\"merge\"]}\n    ]\n    assert report[\"phases\"] == [\n        {\"phase\": \"CONTRACT-READ\", \"entered_at\": \"2026-10-20T08:00:01.000Z\", \"seconds\": 9.0}\n    ]\n\n\ndef test_unstamped_log_reports_starts_without_durations() -> None:\n    line = json.dumps(\n        {\"type\": \"item.started\", \"item\": {\"id\": \"c1\", \"type\": \"command_execution\",\n                                         \"command\": \"python3 -m pytest tests\"}}\n    )\n    report = timing.command_timing(line)\n\n    assert report[\"stamped\"] is False\n    assert {\"muse\": \"/usr/bin/muse\"}\n    resolved[\"prelaunch\"][\"enabled\"] = True\n\n    blocker = task_run_prelaunch.run_prelaunch_gates(\n        payload, resolved, \"brief\", brief_critic=lambda **_kwargs: _review([])\n    )\n\n    assert blocker is None\n    critique = payload[\"prelaunch\"][\"brief_critique\"]\n    assert critique[\"executor\"] == \"codex\"\n    assert
